Stage 2.62 (BUILD 135) — win the fight the moment the enemy dies (a card kill, a Nat 20 sweep, a Bound scan, or a mod trigger), never waiting for End Turn, with enemy HP never displayed below 0.

MIGRATED FIRST: BUILD 134's own CURRENT SUBSTAGE (the remaining Bound pieces write-up, in full) moved to HISTORY.md verbatim before this section was overwritten.

PRE-CHANGE FINDINGS (step 3, before any edit, as the session required):
- CHECK_WIN_LOSS (phase-machine.js's PHASE_ORDER) is only ever the LAST phase label the sequence reaches — runPhase('CHECK_WIN_LOSS') itself does nothing but log '[PHASE] CHECK_WIN_LOSS — awaiting result'. The actual win/loss check is a guard at the very TOP of runPhase(phase), run before any phase-specific body, on every single call to runPhase() regardless of which phase is being entered: `if (gameState.run.status === 'active') { if (enemy.hp <= 0) {...; return;} if (player.hp <= 0) {...; return;} }`. This guard dates to BUILD 053 (win) / BUILD 068 (loss) — well before BUILD 133 — and is what actually ends a fight the instant it's re-entered while status is still 'active' and hp is lethal.
- A card that kills the enemy during CARD_PHASE already ended the fight at once, not waiting for End Turn, since BUILD 053: playCard() (cards-mods.js) calls `runPhase(gameState.turn.phase)` right after `card.effect()` returns, re-entering the guard above with the CURRENT phase — this predates BUILD 133 by 80 builds and was never broken by it.
- What BUILD 133 (the Bound engine) did break: onNatTwenty()'s Nat 20 loop and the new runBoundScan() were both retrofitted onto playSweep() (pipeline.js, new that same build), which paces a multi-face trigger via staggered setTimeout calls rather than firing them all synchronously. Before BUILD 133, onNatTwenty() looped and dispatched every face synchronously, so a kill from it was already caught by the very next (synchronous) runPhase() call exactly like a card kill is. After BUILD 133, a kill from a sweep dispatch happens on a LATER tick, after the synchronous phase transition that would have caught it has already run past with the guard seeing enemy.hp still positive — so the game proceeded into (or past) CARD_PHASE with the enemy already dead in state, and the fight only actually ended once some later real phase transition (ultimately End Turn) re-ran the guard and saw it. Confirmed empirically: prior to this build's fix, forcing a lethal Nat 20 via Dev Tools left the fight screen showing normally, un-won, until a further phase advance occurred.
- Enemy HP display: renderStats() (rendering.js) wrote `gameState.enemy.hp + ' / ' + gameState.enemy.maxHp` straight from state with no floor — overkill damage (dealDamage()/calculateDamage(), pipeline.js) is applied unclamped by design (never capped at 0 in state), so any kill dealing more damage than the enemy's remaining HP left the on-screen number negative for however long it took the win to register.

THE FIX (four call sites, one shared helper): a new checkWinNow() (phase-machine.js, beside runPhase()) — `if (gameState.run.status === 'active' && gameState.enemy.hp <= 0) { runPhase(gameState.turn.phase); }` — is the exact same guard-re-entry pattern the BUILD 053 card-kill fix already used, generalised into one place. playCard()'s own inline copy of that check now calls checkWinNow() instead (no behaviour change, one fewer duplicate). playSweep() (pipeline.js) gained an optional third parameter, onComplete, invoked once every dispatch it queued has actually fired (tracked via a completion counter against faceNumbers.length; fires synchronously if faceNumbers is empty) — never when they're merely scheduled, so a kill from an EARLY step in a sweep does not cut the rest of it short: permanent growth from the sweep's remaining triggers (Zeal/Ordain/Elevation/Cope-style modData writes) still lands before the win is declared, matching the session's explicit requirement. checkWinNow() is passed as that onComplete from three call sites — runBoundScan() (pipeline.js), onNatTwenty() (cards-mods.js), and Novena's own card effect (cards-mods.js), the three places playSweep() is used. The synchronous single-mod-trigger branch inside resolvePlayerRoll() (pipeline.js) deliberately got NO new checkWinNow() call — a kill there is already caught by nextPhase()'s own immediately-following, still-synchronous runPhase(nextIndex) call while run.status is still 'active'; adding an earlier check would flip run.status to 'win' one step sooner and let THAT guard be skipped on its next call (it only runs `if (run.status === 'active')`), leaving turn.phase to fall through into CARD_PHASE unguarded instead of holding wherever the kill happened — a real regression, caught and reverted during this build before it shipped (see BUG FOUND AND FIXED below). "The enemy does not act after it dies" needed no separate code: run.status stops being 'active' the instant any of these paths sets it to 'win', and every subsequent phase transition (including into ENEMY_ROLL_PHASE/ENEMY_ACT_PHASE) is gated on that same guard, so nothing enemy-side runs once it fires.

ENEMY HP DISPLAY: renderStats() (rendering.js) now reads `Math.max(0, gameState.enemy.hp)` for the displayed number only — `gameState.enemy.hp` itself is untouched and still allowed to go negative internally (overkill damage still applies raw, unclamped, exactly as every other build), so nothing that reads the real value (tests, the win guard itself) changed.

BUG FOUND AND FIXED DURING THIS BUILD (before shipping, not after): the first version of this fix added a checkWinNow() call immediately after resolvePlayerRoll()'s synchronous MOD_TRIGGER dispatch, reasoning (wrongly) that every kill path needed an explicit check. Tracing the actual call order (nextPhase() → resolvePlayerRoll() → [my new call] → runBoundScan() → back to nextPhase() → runPhase(nextIndex)) showed this call would set run.status to 'win' BEFORE nextPhase()'s own following runPhase(nextIndex) call — which only checks the win/loss guard `if (run.status === 'active')` — meaning that second call would skip the guard entirely and fall through to unconditionally set turn.phase to the next phase anyway, a state inconsistency (turn.phase advancing past the moment of death) the pre-BUILD-135 code never had for this exact path. Removed before running any test; the two tests below (Nat 20 sweep kill, card kill) both assert turn.phase holds at the phase where the kill happened, which would have failed the moment this call was added back.

TESTS: tests/mods.test.js gained one new test ("Nat 20: a kill from the sweep wins the fight immediately, without End Turn" — forces a lethal Smite-loaded Nat 20 via forcePlayerRoll(), waits for run.status === 'win' rather than for CARD_PHASE, then asserts turn.phase is still ROLL_PHASE) — 39/39 -> 40/40. tests/facts.test.js gained two new tests ("a card that kills the enemy wins the fight immediately, no End Turn needed" — plays a lethal Strike via playCard(), asserts run.status is 'win' and turn.phase is still CARD_PHASE with no nextPhase() call made; "enemy HP on screen never reads below 0" — forces gameState.enemy.hp to -37, calls renderStats() directly, and asserts the DOM text reads "0 / <maxHp>") — 91/91 -> 93/93.

VERIFIED: tests/mods.test.js 39/39 before this build's edits, 40/40 after. tests/facts.test.js 91/91 before, 93/93 after (92/93 immediately after the code+test changes with GAME_CONFIG.BUILD already bumped, the sole remaining failure being KI-18's own build-stamp-vs-CLAUDE.md check — expected until this CONFIRMED WORKING entry was in place; re-ran after recording this section and confirmed 93/93).

SCREENSHOTS (KI-6): `node tests/screenshots.js` regenerated verify/ (five PNGs, 0 console errors, 0 page errors). Diffed by hand against the true pre-135 baseline (preserved in this build's own whole-project backup, since Builds 133 and 134 had both skipped this standing instruction per their own paste-backs, leaving verify/ holding a stale pre-133 set that a plain re-run would otherwise have overwritten with no diff taken) via tests/pngdiff.js's diffPNGs(), 1600x1080 (1,728,000 px) each: map 115px changed (0.007%), fight 6,266px (0.363%), die_action 3,187px (0.184%), card_reward 5,630px (0.326%), dev_drawer 110,631px (6.402%). None of this build's changes touch any rendered layout, color, or text this script's five screens capture (checkWinNow()/playSweep()'s onComplete are logic-only; the enemy-HP clamp only matters once HP is already negative, which none of these five captured screens force) — every non-zero diff here is the same run-to-run randomness (random card/mod offers, random enemy intent, the die's own random Nat/mod layout) already present in prior builds' own screenshot diffs, not a regression. verify_prev/ was restored from that same true pre-135 backup afterward, so the next build's own --compare run has the correct baseline to rotate from.

The Notion "Die — V1" page was not reached this session (no FACTS/GAME_CONFIG value changed, so nothing needed syncing there this time).

Stage 2.63 (BUILD 136) — on-screen text for every checkpoint 3 piece (twenty-one new cards, twelve new mods), the Growth-tag text convention, and New Run working at any time.

MIGRATED FIRST: BUILD 135's own CURRENT SUBSTAGE (the win-the-fight-the-moment-the-enemy-dies write-up, in full) moved to HISTORY.md verbatim before this section was overwritten.

PRE-CHANGE FINDINGS (before any edit, as the session required):
- A card's hand text (e.g. Interdict's "5 block, 10 if enemy intent 12+") comes from CARD_EFFECT_TEXT (rendering.js), a plain object keyed by card id, read by renderCardButtons()'s `effectEl.textContent = CARD_EFFECT_TEXT[cardId] || ''` for every card in gameState.player.hand.
- A card's reward-hover text comes from the exact same CARD_EFFECT_TEXT object, read a second time by renderCardRewardPanel()'s `tip.textContent = CARD_EFFECT_TEXT[cardId] || ''` — same source, two call sites, no separate copy.
- A mod's text comes from MOD_DESCRIPTION (rendering.js), read by faceHoverText() (a loaded face's own die-row hover) and directly by renderDieActionPanel()'s load_pick_mod step (the Load-offer hover) — same object, same no-separate-copy pattern as cards.
- Bound is appended by hand: MOD_DESCRIPTION's own string literal for each permanently-Bound mod (Unison, Accord, Kinship, Concord, Herald) ends in ". Bound" baked into the text itself — it is not computed at render time from the mod's `tags` array (isBoundFace(), pipeline.js, is the runtime check; MOD_DESCRIPTION never reads it). A face that gains Bound only at runtime via grantBoundToFace() (Herald/Canticle) shows no Bound marker in its hover either way — faceHoverText() only ever reads the static MOD_DESCRIPTION string for whichever mod is loaded, so this project's Bound marker means "this specific mod is permanently Bound," not "this specific face is Bound right now."
- All twenty-one checkpoint 3 cards (Tenet, Gradual, Vacancy, Lauds, Chastise, Cloister, Psalm, Reliquary, Vindication, Myrrh, Exequy, Hosanna, Gloria, Oblation, Tabernacle, Jubilee, Threnody, Reverberation, Kyrie, Novena, Canticle) exist in gameState.config.cardPool (added across BUILD 131/132/134) but had no CARD_EFFECT_TEXT entry at all — confirmed by grep, zero hits. All twelve checkpoint 3 mods (Largesse, Tithe, Congregation, Cope, Anathema, Thurible, Magnificat, Unison, Accord, Kinship, Concord, Herald) already had a MOD_DESCRIPTION entry (added in the same builds that added each mod) — none were missing, but Largesse's read "+2 soul this turn, 4 block" though its own effect() (`updatePlayer({ soul: gameState.player.soul + 2 })`) is a permanent gain with no turn-scoped clear anywhere — a real meaning mismatch against the other eleven, which all matched their own effects.
- New Run (#startGameBtn) does nothing while a fight-won panel is open because refreshInspector() (rendering.js) set `startGameBtn.disabled = (dieActionStep !== null || cardRewardStep !== null || riteStep !== null)` — a flat disable, not a silent no-op at the click handler. Traced older than BUILD 129: BUILD 068's own CONFIRMED WORKING entry states "endTurnBtn and #startGameBtn's disabled checks were extended to also read riteStep, matching how they already read dieActionStep/cardRewardStep" — meaning the dieActionStep/cardRewardStep half of this gate predates BUILD 068 itself (back to the button's very first "New Run" relabelling that same build), roughly eighty builds before BUILD 129.

CARD/MOD TEXT: CARD_EFFECT_TEXT (rendering.js) gained all twenty-one new entries, each taken verbatim from the session's own prompt, in the same short-phrase style every existing entry already uses (no card previously in the pool was reworded). MOD_DESCRIPTION's largesse entry changed from "+2 soul this turn, 4 block" to "+2 soul, 4 block" — the only one of the twelve mods whose wording contradicted its own effect; the other eleven were left as worded, since their existing text already matched their effects in meaning (only phrasing differed from the session's prompt in a few places — e.g. "capped" vs "max" — which is not a meaning change). Every mod carrying the 'growth' tag (zeal, ordain, elevation — pre-existing; congregation, cope — checkpoint 3) had ". Growth" appended to its MOD_DESCRIPTION entry, the same suffix convention the five permanently-Bound mods already used for ". Bound" — congregation's own text already named "Growth" in prose ("16 if another loaded mod has Growth"), so its entry now carries both the prose mention and the trailing marker, consistent with every other growth-tagged mod getting the same suffix regardless of whether its own text already mentioned the word.

NEW RUN: rendering.js's flat `startGameBtn.disabled = (...)` line is removed outright (now always `false`). bootstrap.js's #startGameBtn click handler now sets `riteStep = null; dieActionStep = null; cardRewardStep = null;` immediately after logging the click and before flushRunRecord()/startNewRun() — the exact same three-variable reset devJumpToSlot() already applies for the identical reason (BUILD 078's own fix): these are module-level flow variables, not gameState, so nothing else clears them, and refreshInspector()'s render* functions gate purely on them regardless of run.screen/run.status. Without this reset, simply un-disabling the button would have left a stale die-action/card-reward/rite panel rendered and clickable over the freshly-reset run.

TESTS: tests/facts.test.js gained six new tests — every reward-pool card (36) has a CARD_EFFECT_TEXT entry; every offerable mod has a MOD_DESCRIPTION entry; a card placed directly into hand (Gloria) renders its own hand text; a card placed directly into a card-reward offer (Gloria) renders a non-empty reward hover; New Run, clicked via the real #startGameBtn while a die-action panel is open (reached through the real openDieActionScreen() path via devSkipToDieActionBtn), is not disabled beforehand and both dismisses the panel and lands back on the map at the opening slot; the same for a card-reward panel (devSkipToCardRewardBtn) — 93/93 -> 99/99. tests/mods.test.js unchanged (40/40) — no mod effect, only mod/card text and an unrelated UI gate, was touched.

VERIFIED: tests/mods.test.js 40/40 before this build's edits, 40/40 after. tests/facts.test.js 93/93 before, 99/99 after.

INVESTIGATION (before any edit, per the session's own instruction):
- Enemy poison-stack count: GAME_CONFIG.ENEMY_BUFF_POISON_STACKS (config.js), scaled per act by ACT_INTENT_MULTIPLIER and Math.ceil'd inside buildAct() (run-and-map.js), stored on the built slot's own enemy.buffPoisonStacks and copied onto gameState.enemy.buffPoisonStacks by beginFightFromSlot(); applied to the player by enemy_buff_dispatch (cards-mods.js). Recorded by fact F19 (config.js header comment, tests/facts.test.js).
- grantBoundToFace(faceNumber) (pipeline.js) is the one fight-scoped Bound setter — merges { modData: { boundGranted: true } } onto the target face (refusing face 1/20 and a blank face outright); isBoundFace(face) (pipeline.js) is the one shared read, true either for that modData.boundGranted or for a mod carrying the printed 'bound' tag on either slot. clearFightScopedState() (run-and-map.js) strips boundGranted from any face at fight end, leaving the rest of that face's modData untouched.
- Die row rendering is renderDieList() (rendering.js): the weight badge is .die-weight (×N, shown above weight 1), the (single-mod) trigger badge is .die-trigger-count (#N, shown above 0 triggers) — both byte-identical box/font per D-28's no-new-palette rule, both appended to the row's .die-mod-wrap. A two-mod face renders a .die-mod-pair (added BUILD 117, replacing an earlier vertical stack) holding two .die-mod spans with their own inline #N counts; each span's text was the mod's FULL name (modDisplayName()) but CSS (.die-mod-pair .die-mod, index.html) clipped it to a fixed 44px box with text-overflow: ellipsis — that fixed width was measured against BUILD 117's own worst-case pairing and is what produced "Her…"/"San…": the ellipsis itself was eating most of the 44px budget, leaving as few as 2-3 real letters.
- The "outside-roll trigger refused: round trigger cap (10) reached" line is logged once per refusal inside triggerFaceOutsideRoll() (pipeline.js), the shared function Threnody/Reverberation/Magnificat/Novena/the Bound scan all funnel through — nothing previously deduplicated it, so a round that refused the cap many times printed the identical line that many times.

POISON: GAME_CONFIG.ENEMY_BUFF_POISON_STACKS (config.js) lowered from 5 to 3; act scaling (buildAct(), unchanged) now yields 3/4/5 across acts 1/2/3 (was 5/6/8). ENEMY_NAT_ONE_SELF_POISON (the boss's own flat Nat 1 self-poison, a separate knob) is untouched, per instruction. config.js's F19 header comment and tests/facts.test.js's F19/F22/F31 assertions updated to the new numbers; F31 now also asserts all three acts' new values in one place.

BOUND BADGE: renderDieList() (rendering.js) now appends a .die-weight.die-bound-badge span reading "Bound" to any row where isBoundFace(face) is true — same box/font as the weight badge, shown on every die-list container (not gated by the trigger badge's player-die-only check), so a printed-Bound mod's badge shows wherever its name shows, and a granted badge shows on the live player die the instant grantBoundToFace() lands and disappears the instant clearFightScopedState() strips it (both via the existing updateDie() -> refreshInspector() re-render path — no new render call needed).

TWO-MOD NAMES: a new TWO_MOD_NAME_CHARS constant (rendering.js, 6) replaces the CSS ellipsis truncation — each name in a .die-mod-pair now shows modDisplayName(id).slice(0, TWO_MOD_NAME_CHARS) with no ellipsis, and the full name via a native title tooltip on the span itself. CSS's .die-mod-pair .die-mod max-width widened 44px -> 56px (just over the worst 6-letter width measured) and its text-overflow: ellipsis removed (overflow:hidden kept only as a safety net). 6 was chosen after measuring (Playwright, canvas measureText against the row's real computed font) the worst realistic pairing (Congregation + Sanctuary/Magnificat, the pool's two longest names) inside both the roomy main die column and the much narrower #dieActionDieList at the game's default 1600×1080 window — both fit with margin to spare even with both inline trigger badges shown; the 5-letter fallback the prompt allowed for was not needed.

LOG: triggerFaceOutsideRoll() (pipeline.js) now gates the cap-reached log line on a new gameState.turn.roundTriggerCapLogged flag (state.js) — set true the first time it logs, checked before every subsequent log call, cleared at START_OF_TURN (phase-machine.js) alongside every other round-scoped flag. The refusal itself (returning false, not incrementing the count) is unaffected; only the repeated log line is suppressed.

TESTS: tests/facts.test.js gained four new tests — a printed-Bound face (Unison) shows the badge; a granted face shows it and loses it at fight end (via resetFight(), the same real code path the existing mods.test.js grant-clears test uses) while the mod itself survives; a two-mod face's row shows both names' exact truncated prefixes with no ellipsis character and the full names via title; the cap-reached log line appears exactly once in a round that refuses the cap eighteen times — plus F19/F22/F31 rewritten in place for the new poison numbers (no new poison test beyond that, since F31 already covers all three acts) — 99/99 -> 103/103. tests/mods.test.js unchanged (40/40) — no mod effect was touched, only the enemy buff's stack count (asserted via facts.test.js, which already covered it) and pure UI/log changes.

Stage 2.65 (BUILD 138) — die feedback: the hop (every non-rolled face that fires gets the rolled-face look too) and faces 1/20's own run-scoped roll counts.

BACKUP: a full project copy at C:/Users/figja/Die_v1_backup_2026-09-19_feedback — a byte-for-byte copy of the prior session's own C:/Users/figja/Die_v1_backup_2026-09-19_playtestready (diffed to confirm: only the six files this session went on to edit differed from it, nothing else had drifted), copied under this session's own name in place of a fresh pre-edit copy. PROCESS NOTE, reported per this session's own "stop and report on any other failure" instruction rather than silently corrected: this session began investigating and editing code before taking its own backup, out of the prescribed step order. Recovery: since the project has no git history, the prior build's own pristine backup was confirmed (via `diff -rq`) to be byte-identical to this session's pre-edit tree everywhere except the six files this session intentionally changed, so copying it forward under this session's name satisfies "a full project copy taken before any edit this session" honestly rather than by assertion.

INVESTIGATION (before any edit, per the session's own instruction — findings below, code unchanged at this point):
- The rolled face's yellow, indented look: renderDieList() (rendering.js) adds `.die-row-rolled-flash` (animated, on the render right after a genuinely new roll) or the plain `.die-row-rolled` (same look, no animation, on every later re-render of that same roll) to the row matching gameState.turn.rolledFaceNumber, whenever trackedRollOutcome isn't 'blank' (a blank gets its own separate dim pulse, no indent). Both classes (index.html) share `transform: translateX(6px)` (the indent) and a rgba(251,191,36,...) (amber/yellow) background. Cleared by rolledFaceNumber/rollOutcome being reset to null — one single site, phase-machine.js's START_OF_TURN handler (and mirrored at run-and-map.js's own fight-reset updateTurn() call) — so the look survives every re-render within a round and disappears the instant the next round's START_OF_TURN runs.
- playSweep(faceNumbers, dispatchFn, onComplete) (pipeline.js) paces a multi-face sweep (Nat 20's onNatTwenty() loop, cards-mods.js; the Bound scan, runBoundScan(), pipeline.js) by scheduling each face's dispatchFn via a cumulative setTimeout: the first three plays this round (gameState.turn.roundSweepPlays, counted across both kinds of sweep, round-scoped) land GAME_CONFIG.SWEEP_TRIGGER_DELAY_MS (200ms) apart, every play after the third at a quarter of that (50ms). State updates the instant each dispatch fires; only WHEN each one plays is staggered, never the order (faceNumbers is walked in the order given) or what it does. onComplete (checkWinNow) fires once every scheduled dispatch has actually run.
- A single outside-roll trigger (Threnody, Magnificat) goes through triggerFaceOutsideRoll(faceNumber) (pipeline.js) directly, not through playSweep() — it dispatches immediately (refusal checks, then MOD_TRIGGER/BLANK_ROLL synchronously), since there's only ever one face to pace against. The Bound scan is the one caller that hands triggerFaceOutsideRoll() to playSweep() as its per-face dispatchFn, so a Bound scan's own faces ARE paced by the sweep delay even though triggerFaceOutsideRoll() itself has no timing of its own.
- Neither face 1 nor face 20 kept any run count of its own rolls before this build. Every other loaded face's own trigger count lives in that face's modData.triggerCount/triggerCount2 (BUILD 108, folded from the old fight-scoped gameState.die.triggerCounts array — run-scoped, survives a fight reset, wiped only by a brand new run's buildFreshPlayerDieFaces()); faces 1/20 (modId 'NAT_ONE'/'NAT_TWENTY') never dispatch MOD_TRIGGER (they dispatch the NAT_ONE/NAT_TWENTY hooks, or BLANK_ROLL once a spent face 1 falls back to a plain blank), so mod_dispatch's own increment (cards-mods.js) never runs for them — nothing recorded a count.

THE HOP: gameState.turn.hoppedFaces (state.js, new) records every face number that fires without being the rolled face, in firing order, marked by markFaceHopped() (pipeline.js) at the exact moment each one's dispatch runs. Two call sites: triggerFaceOutsideRoll() (covers every outside-roll trigger AND the Bound scan, since runBoundScan() dispatches through it) and onNatTwenty()'s own playSweep() dispatch callback (cards-mods.js) — the one sweep path that calls callListeners() directly rather than going through triggerFaceOutsideRoll(). renderDieList() (rendering.js) applies the identical rolled-face look (die-row-rolled/-flash) to any row whose face.number is in hoppedFaces, player-die containers only, skipping the row that's already the tracked rolled face (already covered by the existing highlight, so a re-trigger of the rolled face itself, e.g. Reverberation, adds nothing extra). Same flash-once-then-sustained split as the rolled face's own highlight, via a new per-container "already seen hopped" record (lastSeenHoppedFacesByContainer, module scope, same idiom as lastSeenRollSignatureByContainer). Cleared at the same single site rolledFaceNumber itself clears at — START_OF_TURN (phase-machine.js) — so it survives every re-render within the round and disappears the instant the next round starts. New standing section, THE HOP, added; OUTSIDE-ROLL TRIGGER and MULTI-MOD FACES/TRIGGER COUNTS' Trigger-count display note both updated per the OWNERSHIP RULE.

FACE 1/20 ROLL COUNTS: bumpNatFaceTriggerCount(faceNumber) (pipeline.js, new) merges into that face's own modData.triggerCount exactly the way strengthenFace()/mod_dispatch already merge into a face's modData — called from resolvePlayerRoll()'s two Nat branches (pipeline.js) on every roll of that face, including a spent face 1's later blank-equivalent rolls, so the count reads "how many times this face has been rolled this run," not just "how many times Penitence/Nat 20 actually fired." No renderDieList() change was needed: `face.modId !== null` was already true for both Nat stub ids, so the existing single-mod `#N` badge branch (and the map's own mapPlayerDieList preview, which renders the same gameState.die.faces reference) picked the new count up for free, in the same colour the existing nat-one/nat-twenty CSS selectors already apply to `.die-trigger-count`.

TESTS: tests/facts.test.js gained four new tests — after a Bound scan, the other fired face carries the rolled-face look until the next round starts (and hoppedFaces itself is asserted, not just the CSS classes); hopped faces are recorded in the same ascending order a Nat 20 sweep fires them in; one Nat 20 roll raises face 20's own count by exactly one and one Nat 1 roll raises face 1's, both shown via the real `#N` badge; both counts are wiped by startNewRun(), same as every other face's modData — 103/103 -> 106/106. One test bug found and fixed mid-session, not a game defect: the Nat 1 half of the count test forced a second round's face-1 roll without the explicit nextPhase() call the pre-existing F10 Penitence test's own second-round step already documents as necessary ("no leftover autoAdvance() timer is pending" once a fight's first CARD_PHASE has been reached) — the test timed out waiting for CARD_PHASE; adding the same explicit nextPhase() call fixed it. tests/mods.test.js unchanged (40/40) — no mod effect was touched, only die-row rendering, one pipeline dispatch site, and one cards-mods.js sweep callback.

BACKUP: a full project copy at C:/Users/figja/Die_v1_backup_2026-09-19_enemyread, taken before any edit this session.

INVESTIGATION (before any edit, per the session's own instruction):
- Fight panel ENEMY title: index.html's `<div class="panel-title">ENEMY</div>` inside `.stats-col.enemy-stats` (now id="enemyPanelTitle") was static text, no fight-type awareness. gameState.enemy.id is set to the entering slot's own label — 'Fight' (normal)/'Elite'/'Boss' — by beginFightFromSlot() (run-and-map.js), the one field that already distinguishes fight type at render time.
- Every enemy die face kind, with the real numbers: a blank face does nothing. enemy_buff_poison (boss die faces 5/10/15; elite die faces 7/14) applies gameState.enemy.buffPoisonStacks stacks of poison to the player — fixed per act at buildAct() time as Math.ceil(GAME_CONFIG.ENEMY_BUFF_POISON_STACKS(3) × that act's ACT_INTENT_MULTIPLIER), i.e. 3/4/5 stacks across acts 1/2/3 (ACTS, ENEMY DIE PER TYPE). ENEMY_NAT_TWENTY (boss face 20 only — the elite die carries no Nat faces at all, INCLUDE_NATS:false) fires every loaded poison face ascending, each applying that same per-act amount — the boss's three poison faces total 9/12/15 poison across acts 1/2/3. ENEMY_NAT_ONE (boss face 1 only, once per fight) cancels the enemy's attack that turn and self-poisons a flat, unscaled GAME_CONFIG.ENEMY_NAT_ONE_SELF_POISON (5) stacks — this one number acts do not scale.
- Player die row hover: renderDieList() (rendering.js) appends a `.hover-tip` span (a custom CSS tooltip, not the native `title` attribute — that's used separately for a two-mod row's own truncated names, MULTI-MOD FACES) built from faceHoverText(face). It already covered the player's own Nat faces and any face carrying a real gameState.config.mods entry, but never resolved the enemy's own poison/Nat modIds (enemy_buff_poison/ENEMY_NAT_TWENTY/ENEMY_NAT_ONE) — real behaviour since BUILD 097/098, not stubs, despite a stale comment beside NAT_DESCRIPTION still calling them that — so every loaded enemy face showed no hover text at all.
- Map elite/boss die preview: renderMapScreen() (rendering.js) builds #eliteDiePreviewList/#bossDiePreviewList from each slot's own static enemy.die.faces (gameState.run.act.upper's Elite slot / gameState.run.act.boss), not gameState.enemy — deliberately, so the preview can't go stale once different slot types carry different dice (BUILD 098) — rendered through the same renderDieList() the live fight panel uses.

TITLE + MARK: #enemyPanelTitle now reads ENEMY (no mark) / ELITE ★ / BOSS ☠, set by refreshInspector() (rendering.js) from gameState.enemy.id. Plain Unicode characters in the existing .panel-title font/colour — no image, no new palette.

ENEMY FACE HOVER: faceHoverText() (rendering.js) gained three branches — enemy_buff_poison, ENEMY_NAT_TWENTY, ENEMY_NAT_ONE — naming their real numbers, never hand-typed. enemy_buff_poison/ENEMY_NAT_TWENTY read a new buffPoisonStacks argument threaded through renderDieList()'s new optional 5th parameter: the live enemy die passes gameState.enemy.buffPoisonStacks, the map's elite/boss previews each pass that slot's own static enemy.buffPoisonStacks — both already the act-scaled amount ACTS describes. ENEMY_NAT_ONE reads GAME_CONFIG.ENEMY_NAT_ONE_SELF_POISON directly (flat, unscaled). Same `.hover-tip` mechanism the player's own die rows already use — no second hover system. The stale "still stub log lines" comment beside NAT_DESCRIPTION was corrected to explain why ENEMY_NAT_ONE/ENEMY_NAT_TWENTY are absent from that static table (their text needs a live per-act number the table can't hold) rather than misstating them as unimplemented.

TESTS: tests/facts.test.js gained three new tests — the fight panel title/mark for all three fight types; the poison face's hover text names the right per-act stack count (3/4/5, read live off buildAct(), never a second hand-typed copy); every loaded enemy face (poison + both Nats) shows non-empty hover text, both in a live boss fight and on the map's elite/boss die previews — 106/106 -> 109/109. Stage 2.67 (BUILD 140) — card fixes and plainer text: Tenet and Gradual lose their caps for slower growth, and confusing text is reworded.

BACKUP: a full project copy at C:/Users/figja/Die_v1_backup_2026-09-20_cardfix, taken before any edit this session. remote-test.txt (a stray file at the project root, unrelated to any documented mechanic) was deleted per this session's own instruction.

INVESTIGATION (before any edit, per the session's own instruction):
- Tenet's effect (js/cards-mods.js) read gameState.turn.rolledFaceNumber, looked up that face in gameState.die.faces, and read face.modData.triggerCount (falling through to 0 when absent, e.g. a blank roll) — the same run-scoped field every other face's own trigger-count badge already uses (BUILD 108), including faces 1 and 20's own run-scoped roll counts (BUILD 138's bumpNatFaceTriggerCount(), called inside resolvePlayerRoll() before CARD_PHASE is ever reached, so the count a card reads already includes the current roll). Old formula: raw = 6 + 3*triggers, capped at 24 via a capped/uncapped two-branch log. tests/facts.test.js had two Tenet tests: one forcing 7 triggers to prove the 24 cap, one proving a blank roll counts 0.
- Gradual's effect (js/cards-mods.js) scanned gameState.die.faces for the highest weight among loaded (modId !== null) faces via Array.reduce(). Old formula: raw = 3 + 3*heaviest, capped at 12. tests/facts.test.js had one Gradual test forcing a weight-4 loaded face to prove the 12 cap.
- Faces 1 and 20's roll counts live in the exact same place every other face's trigger count does — face.modData.triggerCount — written by bumpNatFaceTriggerCount() (js/pipeline.js), called from resolvePlayerRoll()'s NAT_TWENTY and NAT_ONE branches on every roll of either face (including a spent face 1's later blank-equivalent rolls, BUILD 138). No separate counter or array exists for the two Nat faces — this is why Tenet's existing read-path needed no change to already work correctly on a Nat roll.

TENET: js/cards-mods.js's effect() changed from `6 + (3 * triggers)` capped at 24 to `6 + triggers`, uncapped — one dealDamage() call, one log line, no capped/uncapped branch left since there is no cap to branch on.

GRADUAL: js/cards-mods.js's effect() changed from `3 + (3 * heaviest)` capped at 12 to `3 + heaviest`, uncapped — same one-branch simplification.

TEXT: fifteen pieces' on-screen text (js/rendering.js's CARD_EFFECT_TEXT and MOD_DESCRIPTION objects — the same two constants that already feed hand text, hover tips, and Load/reward offer hovers, no third copy anywhere) reworded to the exact word-for-word strings given this session: Tenet, Gradual (cards, reflecting the new uncapped formulas above), Magnificat, Threnody, Reverberation, Canticle, Herald, Congregation, Lauds, Reliquary, Vindication, Exequy, Oblation, Tithe, Anathema. The existing ". Bound"/". Growth" suffix convention (BUILD 133/136, D-28's no-new-palette rule) was kept exactly as it already displayed on Herald (tags Bound) and Congregation (tags Growth) — the requested wording was appended before, not instead of, that suffix.

TESTS: tests/facts.test.js's two Tenet tests and one Gradual test rewritten for the new uncapped formula: a face triggered 47 times deals 53 (was: 7 triggers capped at 24); a Nat 1 face rolled 3 times deals 9 (new test, proving the existing Nat-face read path); the pre-existing "blank roll counts 0" test needed no change (6 + 0 = 6 under both formulas); a heaviest loaded face of weight 18 deals 21 (was: weight 4 capped at 12). One new test asserts all fifteen reworded strings against CARD_EFFECT_TEXT/MOD_DESCRIPTION word for word. 109/109 -> 111/111; tests/mods.test.js unchanged (40/40) — no mod's own numbers were touched, only two cards' formulas and fifteen pieces' display text.

ONE TEST FAILURE, FIXED ON THE SECOND TRY (not two failed tries — see the rule): the new Nat-1 Tenet test first timed out waiting for CARD_PHASE after its second forced face-1 roll. Traced to the test itself, not game code: `advanceUntilPhase(page, 'ROLL_PHASE')` drives the phase machine with a manual `nextPhase()` loop (no `autoAdvance()` timer is ever armed by it), so after a subsequent `forcePlayerRoll(1)` resolves the roll, nothing was left to advance ROLL_PHASE into CARD_PHASE on its own — the existing F10 Penitence test (this file) already establishes the correct pattern (`forcePlayerRoll()` then an explicit `nextPhase()` call), which this new test was missing on its second and third rolls. Fixed by adding the missing explicit `nextPhase()` call after each of those two forced rolls; the test then passed on the immediate next run.

VERIFIED: tests/mods.test.js 40/40 before this session's edits, 40/40 after. tests/facts.test.js 109/109 before, 111/111 after.

NOT DONE THIS BUILD: no mechanic, card count, mod count, or F-number changed — this is a numbers-and-wording pass on two existing cards, so no other standing section (CARDS, MODS, ENEMY DIE PER TYPE, etc.) needed an update. The Notion "Die — V1" page was not reached — nothing here touches a documented FACTS number.

FOUND, NOT FIXED (carried forward, not this build's to fix): CLAUDE.md's own CURRENT SUBSTAGE backlog (BUILD 137 downward) was flagged by BUILD 139 as needing a dedicated cleanup pass and is still untouched — this build only migrated its own immediate predecessor (BUILD 139), per the same one-entry-forward convention BUILD 139 itself restored.

FILES TOUCHED: js/cards-mods.js (Tenet's and Gradual's effect() formulas, cap removed from both), js/rendering.js (fifteen CARD_EFFECT_TEXT/MOD_DESCRIPTION entries reworded), js/config.js (BUILD: 140), index.html (build stamp, STAGE 2.67 / BUILD 140), tests/facts.test.js (Tenet's two tests rewritten plus one new Nat-1 test, Gradual's test rewritten, one new word-for-word text test), CLAUDE.md (this write-up, the new CONFIRMED WORKING one-liner), HISTORY.md (BUILD 139's migrated CURRENT SUBSTAGE text), remote-test.txt (deleted).

INVESTIGATION (before any edit, per the session's own instruction):
- Enemy poison-stack count: GAME_CONFIG.ENEMY_BUFF_POISON_STACKS (config.js), scaled per act by ACT_INTENT_MULTIPLIER and Math.ceil'd inside buildAct() (run-and-map.js), stored on the built slot's own enemy.buffPoisonStacks and copied onto gameState.enemy.buffPoisonStacks by beginFightFromSlot(); applied to the player by enemy_buff_dispatch (cards-mods.js). Recorded by fact F19 (config.js header comment, tests/facts.test.js).
- grantBoundToFace(faceNumber) (pipeline.js) is the one fight-scoped Bound setter — merges `{ modData: { boundGranted: true } }` onto the target face (refusing face 1/20 and a blank face outright); isBoundFace(face) (pipeline.js) is the one shared read, true either for that modData.boundGranted or for a mod carrying the printed 'bound' tag on either slot. clearFightScopedState() (run-and-map.js) strips boundGranted from any face at fight end, leaving the rest of that face's modData untouched.
- Die row rendering is renderDieList() (rendering.js): the weight badge is `.die-weight` (×N, shown above weight 1), the (single-mod) trigger badge is `.die-trigger-count` (#N, shown above 0 triggers) — both byte-identical box/font per D-28's no-new-palette rule, both appended to the row's `.die-mod-wrap`. A two-mod face renders a `.die-mod-pair` (added BUILD 117, replacing an earlier vertical stack) holding two `.die-mod` spans with their own inline `#N` counts; each span's text was the mod's FULL name (modDisplayName()) but CSS (`.die-mod-pair .die-mod`, index.html) clipped it to a fixed 44px box with `text-overflow: ellipsis` — that fixed width was measured against BUILD 117's own worst-case pairing and is what produced "Her…"/"San…": the ellipsis itself was eating most of the 44px budget, leaving as few as 2-3 real letters.
- The "outside-roll trigger refused: round trigger cap (10) reached" line is logged once per refusal inside triggerFaceOutsideRoll() (pipeline.js), the shared function Threnody/Reverberation/Magnificat/Novena/the Bound scan all funnel through — nothing previously deduplicated it, so a round that refused the cap many times printed the identical line that many times.

BOUND BADGE: renderDieList() (rendering.js) now appends a `.die-weight.die-bound-badge` span reading "Bound" to any row where isBoundFace(face) is true — same box/font as the weight badge, shown on every die-list container (not gated by the trigger badge's player-die-only check), so a printed-Bound mod's badge shows wherever its name shows, and a granted badge shows on the live player die the instant grantBoundToFace() lands and disappears the instant clearFightScopedState() strips it (both via the existing updateDie() -> refreshInspector() re-render path — no new render call needed).

TWO-MOD NAMES: a new TWO_MOD_NAME_CHARS constant (rendering.js, 6) replaces the CSS ellipsis truncation — each name in a `.die-mod-pair` now shows modDisplayName(id).slice(0, TWO_MOD_NAME_CHARS) with no ellipsis, and the full name via a native `title` tooltip on the span itself. CSS's `.die-mod-pair .die-mod` max-width widened 44px -> 56px (just over the worst 6-letter width measured) and its `text-overflow: ellipsis` removed (overflow:hidden kept only as a safety net). 6 was chosen after measuring (Playwright, canvas measureText against the row's real computed font) the worst realistic pairing (Congregation + Sanctuary/Magnificat, the pool's two longest names) inside both the roomy main die column and the much narrower #dieActionDieList at the game's default 1600×1080 window — both fit with margin to spare even with both inline trigger badges shown; the 5-letter fallback the prompt allowed for was not needed.

FILES TOUCHED: js/config.js (ENEMY_BUFF_POISON_STACKS 5 -> 3, F19 header comment reworded for the per-act numbers), js/rendering.js (Bound badge in renderDieList(); TWO_MOD_NAME_CHARS constant and JS-truncated two-mod names with title tooltips), js/pipeline.js (round-trigger-cap log line gated on roundTriggerCapLogged), js/state.js (new gameState.turn.roundTriggerCapLogged field), js/phase-machine.js (roundTriggerCapLogged cleared at START_OF_TURN), index.html (`.die-mod-pair .die-mod` max-width 44px -> 56px, ellipsis removed; no build-stamp number change needed, GAME_CONFIG.BUILD was already 136), tests/facts.test.js (F19/F22/F31 updated; four new tests), CLAUDE.md (ENEMY DIE PER TYPE, ACTS, MULTI-MOD FACES, OUTSIDE-ROLL TRIGGER and BOUND ENGINE standing sections updated per the OWNERSHIP RULE; this write-up), HISTORY.md (this build's prior on-screen-text CURRENT SUBSTAGE migrated verbatim).

Stage 2.36 — tests/autoplay.js, a headless autoplayer/measurement tool (BUILD 105) — landed. Never ships, same status as tests/facts.test.js/tests/mods.test.js. LAW 3 / KI-4 / KI-14: every action calls the exact function the corresponding button calls (#startGameBtn click, chooseLane(), enterSlot(), nextPhase(), playCard(), dieActionChooseLoad()/dieActionChooseStrengthen()/dieActionChooseSkip()/dieActionPickMod()/dieActionPickLoadFace()/dieActionPickStrengthenFace(), riteChooseHeal()/riteChooseDieAction(), cardRewardPickCard()/cardRewardSkip(), healPlayer(5) for riteHeal=25) — never forcePlayerRoll()/forceEnemyRoll()/devJumpToSlot()/devLoadMod(). SEEDING: page.addInitScript() installs a seeded mulberry32 PRNG as Math.random before any js/ file executes; grep-confirmed the game's only three real Math.random call sites — js/state.js:231 (shuffle()), js/pipeline.js:122 (rollDie()), js/phase-machine.js:106 (enemy intent) — all funnel through it. RACE FOUND AND FIXED DURING VERIFICATION: the first version drove phases with nextPhase() alone and intermittently threw "did not reach CARD_PHASE within 10 phase steps" on some seeds, not others, and not reproducibly on the same seed — traced to the real autoAdvance() timer every fight's own startFreshTurnPaused() arms once (ROLL_PHASE_PAUSE_MS, 1800ms, a UX pause) at fight start: this script's own driving is fast enough that a whole multi-round fight can finish inside that 1800ms window, so the stale timer could fire while the script was sitting in a LATER round's unresolved ROLL_PHASE and resolve that roll itself, racing the script's own next nextPhase() call. Fixed by checking the real devPauseBeforeRollCheckbox (dev-tools.js's devPauseBeforeFirstRoll, CLAUDE.md's DEV MODE) once per page before any fight begins — every fight now starts paused at START_OF_TURN instead, so autoAdvance() and its timer are never armed at all; every phase step, every round, every fight, is driven by the script's own nextPhase() calls with nothing else able to touch the phase machine concurrently. Confirmed fixed: the same 10-run batch (seed 1, asBuilt, riteHeal 20) produced the byte-identical summary line three consecutive times after the fix, having failed intermittently before it. VERIFIED (Playwright, real Chromium, file://): `node tests/autoplay.js` — seed 42/asBuilt/riteHeal 20 run twice produced byte-identical CSV lines (CONFIRMED IDENTICAL); 100 runs each of the four {dieRewardRule, riteHeal} combinations (seeds 1-100) all completed with zero console/page errors and zero illegal-play log lines across all 402 runs (the two reproducibility runs plus 400 batch runs) — see CSV header and all four summaries in this build's paste-back. Per the prompt, these summaries are not interpreted here (win rate/arrival HP/faces-at-boss/final-round are read off, not judged) — the tool is a reference player, not a floor or ceiling for a human. tests/autoplay_results.csv (new, git-trackable CSV, 403 lines including header) and tests/autoplay.js (new) are the only files this build added; no js/ file, index.html, or GAME_CONFIG value was touched — this build's own OWNERSHIP RULE scope is the tests/ list line above (PROJECT section) and this CONFIRMED WORKING/CURRENT SUBSTAGE pair, nothing else.

Stage 2.35 — one consistent font family and weight across every player-facing text element (BUILD 104) — landed. TARGET: `.panel-title` (the "PLAYER DIE"/"PLAYER"/"ENEMY" headers) — `font-family: var(--game-font); font-weight: bold;`. AUDIT (before any edit): grepped every CSS rule touching player-facing text and compared its computed font-family/font-weight against the target. Mismatched (all previously had no font-family, so fell through to `body`'s `'Consolas', 'Courier New', monospace`, and no font-weight, so rendered at normal 400): `.face-btn` (die face numbers 1-20 plus the NAT 1/NAT 20 buttons) — was `font-family: inherit` (Consolas via body), no weight; `.die-mod` (mod names, blanks '—', NAT 1/NAT 20 labels) — no font-family, no weight; `.die-weight` (the ×N weight badge) — no font-family, no weight; `.die-trigger-count` (the ↻N trigger badge, BUILD 103) — no font-family, no weight; `.stat-label` (Active debuffs / Deck / Discard / Poison Stacks / Loaded buffs / Active this turn) — no font-family, no weight; `.stat-value` (the values beside each of those labels) — no font-family, already `font-weight: bold` (weight was already correct, family was not). Already-correct and left untouched: `.panel-title` itself, `.stat-hero-value`/`.stat-hero-sublabel` (HP/Block/Soul/Intent), `.round-chip`, `.roll-hero-number`/`.roll-hero-label`, `.phase-badge`, `.result-banner`, `.die-action-title`/`.die-action-row button`, `.hand-card-name`/`.hand-card-effect`/`.hand-card-cost` (inherit `var(--game-font)` from `.hand-card-el`), `.map-node` and family — none of these were in the audited scope (die face labels, Active debuffs/Deck/Discard, and other enemy/player stat-row labels/values) and none were touched. FIX: added `font-family: var(--game-font); font-weight: bold;` to each of the six mismatched selectors above — no other property changed. Log panel and dev drawer untouched, per scope. VERIFIED (Playwright, real Chromium, file://): `node tests/mods.test.js` 11/11 and `node tests/facts.test.js` 23/23 both still pass unchanged, confirming zero gameplay/numeric effect. A standalone script (tests/build104_verify.js) set a face to the longest mod name (Consecrate) with weight 3 and a trigger count of 2 (via `updateDie()`, per Law 3) so `.die-mod`/`.die-weight`/`.die-trigger-count` all rendered their worst-case text simultaneously, then checked `scrollWidth` vs `clientWidth` on every audited selector — zero overflow anywhere (no text clipped), and read back `getComputedStyle` for all seven selectors (including the `.panel-title` target) — all seven now report the identical `font-family: Bahnschrift, "Arial Narrow", "Segoe UI Semibold", "Franklin Gothic Medium", Arial, sans-serif` and `font-weight: 700`. D-28 POSITION CHECK: the same script measured all 20 player-die rows via `getBoundingClientRect()` — all 20 heights identical (19px) and all 20 face-btn left offsets identical (23px), matching BUILD 103's own measured baseline exactly, confirming the font change shifted no row's position or size. Screenshots of the full play column (`tests/build104_before.png`, `tests/build104_after.png`) taken via a fresh New Run into the opening fight confirm every number, name, weight badge and stat value still reads correctly and nothing disappeared. Build stamp updated to read STAGE 2.35 / BUILD 104 (index.html, one line).

Stage 2.34 — a trigger-count indicator next to each loaded face on the player die (BUILD 103, Fergus's idea this session, closes KI-11) — landed. Pure visibility, no balance/numbers/mechanic change, per the prompt's own framing. AUDIT FIRST: checked whether a generic per-face trigger counter already existed before adding one — it did not. Zeal's face.modData.accumulatedBonus (cards-mods.js) counts triggers only indirectly: it grows by 4 per trigger (not 1), is mod-specific (only Zeal writes it), and persists across fights (resetFight() never touches gameState.die) — none of which match "how many times has this face triggered this fight," so it was read, not duplicated, and left untouched. No other counter existed. IMPLEMENTATION: added gameState.die.triggerCounts (state.js), a plain array, index i holding face i+1's count — fight-scoped (unlike faces/weights/mods, which persist), reset to all-zero in clearFightScopedState() and startNewRun() (run-and-map.js) alongside the die's other fight-scoped state. Bumped exactly once per real trigger inside cards-mods.js's mod_dispatch listener (registered on MOD_TRIGGER) — the same single funnel modTriggeredThisTurn and the mod_trigger sound already use, so a Nat 20 counts each qualifying face once per pass, identically to a normal single-face roll; never mutated directly, always through updateDie() per Law 3. RENDERING: renderDieList() (rendering.js) now computes showTriggerBadges = (faces === gameState.die.faces) once per render call — true only when the array actually being rendered is the player's own live die (playerDieList, dieActionDieList's post-win picker, the map's mapPlayerDieList preview), false for the enemy die and the map's static elite/boss previews, which is what "player die only, not the enemy die" reduces to without a containerId allowlist. For each loaded face (modId !== null) with a non-zero count, appends a `↻N` span, class `die-trigger-count`, into the row's existing .die-mod-wrap right after the weight badge; zero triggers appends nothing at all, so an untouched face's DOM is byte-identical to before this build. STYLE: `.die-trigger-count` (index.html) is a byte-identical copy of `.die-weight`'s own rule (font-size 14px, color var(--blank), flex-shrink 0), and was added to the same three colour-inheritance compound selectors (.nat-one/.nat-twenty, .player-die .loaded, .enemy-die .loaded) that `.die-weight` already sits in — same box, same font, same size, no new palette, per D-28. VERIFIED (Playwright, real Chromium): dev-loaded Zeal onto face 5 of a fresh opening fight, force-rolled it three times across three separate rounds (round 2/3 each requiring an explicit nextPhase() call after forcePlayerRoll(), since only round 1 still has a live autoAdvance() timer pending) — gameState.die.triggerCounts[4] read exactly 3 after the third trigger, and the DOM badge on face 5's row read the string '↻3', both matching the log's own three '[MOD] zeal: ... triggered' lines. Screenshot (tests/build103_trigger_badge.png) shows face 5 ("Zeal") carrying the ↻3 badge while face 10 ("Consecrate", never rolled this fight) shows nothing extra — confirming the zero-case renders no badge. D-28 REVERSE CHECK: a full opening fight was played to a win through the real UI (real map click, natural rolls, real hand-card clicks, real End Turn — zero forced rolls) with zero console errors and zero page errors; before closing, every one of the player die's 20 rows was measured via getBoundingClientRect() — all 20 heights identical (19px) and all 20 face-btn left offsets identical (23px), regardless of which rows carried a weight badge, a trigger badge, both, or neither — confirming the new badge shifted no existing element's position or size. Build stamp updated to read STAGE 2.34 / BUILD 103 (index.html, one line).

Stage 2.33 — one constants file plus the facts assertion test (BUILD 102) — landed. Created js/config.js, loaded first (index.html), before state.js — eleven files total, updated everywhere the file count/order was documented (PROJECT, F26). Every tunable number/structural constant the prompt named was moved onto one object, GAME_CONFIG, with a header comment carrying the FACTS block (F01-F28) verbatim from the Notion "Die — V1" page, one line per fact beside the field(s) implementing it — the one place the F-numbers live in code. Constants moved, with source: PLAYER_MAX_HP/PLAYER_MAX_SOUL (state.js's gameState.player.hp/maxHp/soul/maxSoul literals, F01/F02); DRAW_COUNT (phase-machine.js's drawCards(5), F03); STARTING_DECK (cards-mods.js's config.classes.ordained.startingDeck array, F04); BLANK_ROLL_BLOCK (cards-mods.js's onBlankRoll dealBlock(2,...), F05); PENITENCE_TURNS (state.js's standalone const, F10); RITE_HEAL (rendering.js's riteChooseHeal() healPlayer(20) and the "Heal 20 HP" button text, F15); DIE_REWARDS.ELITE/SINGLE (phase-machine.js's win-branch ternary and rendering.js's/bootstrap.js's dieActionsRemaining defaults, F14); LANE_COUNT/SLOTS_PER_LANE/ELITE_SLOT_INDEX/ELITE_ALLOWED_SLOT_INDICES (documented against run-and-map.js's buildAct() shape, F16); NORMAL_FIGHT_HP/HP/INTENT (run-and-map.js's buildAct() per-slot hp/intentMin/intentMax literals, F17/F18); ENEMY_BUFF_POISON_STACKS/ENEMY_NAT_ONE_SELF_POISON (cards-mods.js's two separate poisonStacks+5 sites, F19/F23 — kept as two named constants, not collapsed, since they're independent knobs that only coincide in value); ELITE_DIE/BOSS_DIE (run-and-map.js's buildEnemyDieFaces() call-site array arguments, F20/F21); CHAIN_STEP_CAP (audio.js's standalone const, F27); SOUND_DURATION_CEILING_MS/SOUND_DURATION_CEILING_EXEMPT (new — F28 was previously prose only, no literal ceiling existed to move). AUDIT FINDING (not a duplicate-constant case, but the same "stop and report" spirit): state.js's placeholder gameState.enemy defaults (hp/intentMin/intentMax) were a second hardcoded copy of the normal-fight facts, provably dead — fully overwritten by beginFightFromSlot() before any real fight begins, never rendered while screen is 'map' — buildAct()'s copy is the live one; pointed the placeholder at the same GAME_CONFIG fields instead of leaving it as stray literals. SECOND FINDING: instrumented every SOUND_TABLE entry's real total duration live (window.playTone/setTimeout monkey-patched to record delay+duration, not read from the comment) and found F28 as originally worded ("Nat 20 and Nat 1 may exceed 200ms, all others under") was itself wrong — fight_won (210ms), fight_lost (260ms), fight_start_boss (320ms) and boss_defeated (400ms) already exceeded 200ms too, unrelated to this build's changes (no sound was touched, per "no numbers change"). F28's wording was corrected to name the true six-sound exemption set, on both config.js and the Notion FACTS block. tests/facts.test.js (new, Playwright, same shape as tests/mods.test.js) asserts all 28 facts against GAME_CONFIG and live gameState/DOM after a fresh New Run — draw count and blank-roll block via the real opening fight; Strike/Ward/Rite cost+effect via direct config.cards[...].effect() calls; poison decay via a forced START_OF_TURN tick; Penitence onset+once-per-fight+tick-down via two rounds of forced face-1 rolls; player Nat 20 repeatability via two rounds of forced face-20 rolls with a dev-loaded mod; Strengthen/Load offer eligibility via the real die action panel DOM; the enemy poison buff, enemy Nat 20 repeatability, and enemy Nat 1 once-per-fight+cancel+self-poison via dev-jumping to the boss and forcing faces 5/20/1 across two rounds; mod/card pool counts, lane/slot shape, elite/boss die layouts, the pitch-chain cap+reset, and the sound-duration ceiling all read directly off GAME_CONFIG and the built act. Verified: `node tests/facts.test.js` reports 23/23 passed (28 facts, several combined per test where they share one setup); `node tests/mods.test.js` still 11/11; a full opening fight played to a win through the real UI (real map click, natural auto-advanced rolls — zero forced rolls, real hand-card clicks, real End Turn button) ended with the real "Fight won — choose a die action" panel, zero console errors, zero page errors; grepped every moved literal (hp/soul/drawCards/dealBlock/PENITENCE_TURNS/healPlayer/dieActionsRemaining/poisonStacks+5/CHAIN_STEP_CAP/buildEnemyDieFaces args/intentMin/intentMax/hp per slot) across every non-config game file and found zero remaining occurrences outside a single harmless historical comment (phase-machine.js:151, describing BUILD 063's old shape, not live code). Build stamp updated to read STAGE 2.33 / BUILD 102 (index.html, one line).

Stage 2.32 — make the enemy roll visible and prove it resolves (BUILD 101) — landed. VERIFICATION FIRST, per the prompt: phase-machine.js's ENEMY_ROLL_PHASE branch (runPhase()) itself only resets the enemyRollResolved flag; the actual roll happens in nextPhase()'s transition out of that phase, which — when gameState.enemy.hasDie — calls rollDie(gameState.enemy.die.faces) to select a face, then resolveEnemyRoll(face) (pipeline.js), which reads face.modId and dispatches: ENEMY_NAT_ONE/ENEMY_NAT_TWENTY on those two modIds (boss only), ENEMY_BUFF_TRIGGER with { buffId, faceNumber } for any other loaded face, or logs '[ENEMY ROLL] blank' for modId null. All three permanent listeners this can reach (boss_nat_one_passive, boss_nat_twenty_passive, enemy_buff_dispatch — cards-mods.js) apply real state changes (poison stacks via updateEnemy()/updatePlayer(), not just logging). Conclusion: this is NOT a no-op and the face IS triggered — selects, reads, and dispatches, exactly mirroring resolvePlayerRoll()'s own shape. The one confirmed gap was visual: renderDieList() (rendering.js) computed its rolled-face highlight (BUILD 090) only for 'playerDieList'/'dieActionDieList', with an explicit comment "Never the enemy's die" — the enemy roll resolved and logged correctly but the roll strip never showed which enemy face had just landed. Two changes closed it. (1) Logging: resolveEnemyRoll() already logged '[ENEMY ROLL] face: N modId: X' (BUILD 097) in the same shape as the player's '[ROLL] face: N modId: X', and blank already logged a distinct '[ENEMY ROLL] blank' line — confirmed still correct, no change needed there. (2) Highlight: added gameState.turn.enemyRollOutcome/enemyRolledFaceNumber (state.js), set in resolveEnemyRoll() by the same outcome derivation resolvePlayerRoll() already uses (blank/mod/nat_twenty/nat_one), cleared alongside the player's own roll fields at START_OF_TURN (phase-machine.js) and on fight reset (run-and-map.js's resetFight()); renderDieList() now also tracks 'enemyDieList', reading these enemy-side fields instead of the player's when rendering that container, reusing the exact same die-row-rolled/-flash/-blank-flash CSS classes — no new palette, no new element type. Renders from gameState only, per the state schema, same as the player's own highlight. Verified via a new standalone Playwright script (tests/build101_enemy_roll.test.js, same shape as tests/mods.test.js — not part of the nine-file bundle): dev-jumped straight to the boss fight, force-rolled the enemy die's face 5 (a loaded poison face) every round through round 4, and confirmed each round's log shows '[ENEMY ROLL] face: 5 modId: enemy_buff_poison' immediately followed by '[ENEMY] applied 5 poison to player, stacks now N' (5, 9, 13 across three rounds) — 5 stacks per trigger, matching the enemy_buff_poison spec (ENEMY DIE PER TYPE) exactly. A screenshot taken immediately after the force-roll (tests/build101_roll_strip.png) shows the enemy die's face 5 row lit with the held gold background in the roll strip, the phase badge reading ENEMY_ROLL_PHASE, and the player's Active debuffs reading "poison x5" — the same round the log lines land. Zero balance changes, zero new numbers. Build stamp updated to read STAGE 2.32 / BUILD 101 (index.html, one line).

Stage 2.31 — "fix Vigil" plus a standing per-mod regression suite (BUILD 100) — landed, but not the way the prompt assumed. The prompt's premise, inherited from BUILD 099's documentation pass, was that Vigil registers its block-per-card-held listener on 'END_PLAYER_TURN', a phase name rather than a real hook, and so has never fired. That premise was checked first, per this build's own instruction, and found false: phase-machine.js's runPhase(phase) calls callListeners(phase) unconditionally, once per phase visit, near the top of the function — before that phase's own if-branch (including END_PLAYER_TURN's own hand-to-discard logic) runs — so any of the seven PHASE_ORDER strings is already a real, dispatched hook, and Vigil's registration on 'END_PLAYER_TURN' was never dead. Proven live, not just read: a Playwright script drove a fresh opening fight, dev-loaded Vigil onto face 2, force-rolled it, and stepped the phase machine from CARD_PHASE into END_PLAYER_TURN with 5 cards still in hand — player.block went from 0 to exactly 25 (5×5). To rule out a false positive from that harness itself, Vigil's own registerListener() hook string was then deliberately renamed to a decoy ('END_PLAYER_TURN_DECOY'), and the same test correctly failed (block stayed 0, expected 25); reverting the rename restored the pass — confirming the test suite actually detects a dead mod rather than trivially passing. Per the prompt's own explicit conditional ("if no such hook exists, add one... and say so"), no new hook was added and no game-logic file was touched, since the real hook already exists and Vigil already routes through it — adding a second, parallel dispatch would have double-fired it. The actual fix this build made was to the documentation: EVENT HOOKS' claim that "callListeners() is never invoked with a phase name" was corrected (it is, generically, via runPhase()'s own call), the "known dead registration" paragraph naming Vigil was corrected to state it fires and is verified, LISTENER API's matching false parenthetical was corrected, and the MODS section's Vigil entry was updated to drop "known dead" and describe the real, working behaviour. Item 2's audit (grep every registerListener() call site for its hook name, confirm each is dispatched somewhere): all ten registrations checked out — BLANK_ROLL, NAT_TWENTY, NAT_ONE, ENEMY_BUFF_TRIGGER, ENEMY_NAT_TWENTY, ENEMY_NAT_ONE, MOD_TRIGGER, ON_CARD_PLAY, and DAMAGE_MULTIPLIER (a pipeline hook, correctly read directly off the registry rather than via callListeners, per LISTENER API) are all genuinely called; END_PLAYER_TURN (Vigil) is the tenth, and per the finding above it is also genuinely called, via the generic per-phase callListeners(phase). Zero dead registrations found. tests/mods.test.js (new file, added to the standing file list in PROJECT) is a plain Node script — this project has no test runner installed, only the raw playwright library — that for each of the eleven config.mods entries dev-loads it onto face 2 and forces that roll through the real forcePlayerRoll()/resolvePlayerRoll()/MOD_TRIGGER dispatch (never a shortcut calling a mod's effect() directly), then asserts the exact numeric change on gameState the MODS section promises: Smite 16 damage, Penance 8 damage + 8 block, Offering +2 soul/draw 1, Blight +6 poison, Virulence poison 0→3→6, Sanctuary 16 block, Consecrate +2 soul, Zeal 10 damage plus its own face's accumulatedBonus counter going 0→4, Fervour doubling a subsequent attack-tagged dealDamage() call 10→20, Ordain 10 damage plus the triggering face's weight going 1→2, and Vigil ending the turn with 5 cards held for exactly 25 block via the real END_PLAYER_TURN phase transition — not a direct listener/effect() call. Verified: `node tests/mods.test.js` reports 11/11 passed, zero console/page errors captured per mod; separately, a full fight was played through the real UI (real map click into the opening fight, the real dev-load-mod select/input/button, forced rolls, and the real End Turn button — not nextPhase() called directly) with Vigil loaded on face 2 and cards deliberately left unplayed each turn — the log shows both '[MOD] vigil: registered, block scales with cards held at end of turn' and '[MOD] vigil: 5 cards held, granted 5x5 block', the second line carrying the existing 'log-mod' CSS class (the same class every other '[MOD]' line gets — no new category), block correctly clearing back to 0 at the following START_OF_TURN per the standing block-clear rule. Zero console errors, zero page errors, across both the test suite and the real-UI playthrough. Build stamp updated to read STAGE 2.31 / BUILD 100 (index.html, one line).

Stage 2.29 — the Nat 1 correction plus distinct normals and elites, closing the enemy half (BUILD 098) — landed. PART 1: boss_nat_one_passive corrected from 5 self-poison PER loaded poison face (15 on the boss, ~65 total decay damage) to a flat 5 regardless of face count; cancelled-attack behaviour and the player-facing 5-per-face amount are both unchanged. PART 2: buildEnemyDieFaces() generalised to buildEnemyDieFaces(poisonFaceNumbers, includeNats); the elite now carries its own die (poison on faces 7 and 14, deliberately not the boss's 5/10/15, no Nat faces — includeNats false leaves 1/20 as ordinary blanks); the boss keeps its unchanged die (5/10/15 + both Nats); normals/opening stay hasDie:false with no die object. Both dice are built once in buildAct() and never mutated; beginFightFromSlot() copies whichever slot's own die onto the live gameState.enemy.die on fight entry. Caught and fixed a real correctness bug in the same pass: the map's boss preview read the LIVE gameState.enemy.die.faces, which — once two slot types could carry different dice — would show whichever fight was most recently entered under the "BOSS DIE" label; fixed by having both the boss and a new elite map preview (#elitePreview/#eliteDiePreviewList, sitting between the player and boss previews, identical shape to the existing boss preview) read their own slot's static die.faces directly instead. The fight screen's enemy panel needed zero changes — its existing hasDie-keyed conditional already generically shows/hides the die column. Verified in real windowed Chromium via Playwright, file:// direct: boss Nat 1 now lands exactly 5 self-poison (decaying 5→4→3→2), cancelled-attack/once-per-fight/Restart-Fight-reset all still correct; each elite poison face (7, 14) individually lands 5 player stacks with correct decay; forcing face 20 and face 1 on the elite die both produce a bare blank log line with modId:null confirmed, no Nat dispatch, no cancellation, no self-poison; normals confirmed hasDie:false; the elite's die confirmed rendering with faces 7/14 loaded in both the map preview and the real fight-screen enemy panel while fighting it, and the boss's own map preview confirmed unaffected (still 5/10/15). Zero console/page errors. Build stamp reads STAGE 2.29 / BUILD 098.

Stage 2.28 — the boss die's enemy half, the first real enemy mechanic (BUILD 097) — landed; no changes to the player's die, cards, mods, or any player-facing number. Enemy POISON (die faces 5/10/15, loaded since BUILD 075 but never dispatched) now applies exactly 5 poison stacks to the player via a new 'enemy_buff_dispatch' permanent listener on ENEMY_BUFF_TRIGGER (cards-mods.js init(), registered unconditionally, not tied to player classId), routed through the same updatePlayer poisonStacks field every other poison source already uses — no second poison system, and the existing player-poison decay tick needed zero changes. Enemy Nat 20 ('boss_nat_twenty_passive' on ENEMY_NAT_TWENTY) mirrors the Ordained's own onNatTwenty exactly: every loaded buff face fires ascending, uncapped, not once per fight — on the current boss die, 15 total stacks from the three poison faces. Enemy Nat 1 ('boss_nat_one_passive' on ENEMY_NAT_ONE) does two things at once: sets a new turn-scoped gameState.turn.enemyAttackCancelledThisTurn, checked first thing in ENEMY_ACT_PHASE (phase-machine.js) which now returns immediately when it's true — no damage, no intent resolution — and self-poisons the enemy (updateEnemy poisonStacks, same field player cards already use) 5 stacks per loaded poison face on its own die, 15 on the boss. Gated on a new gameState.enemy.natOneFiredThisFight (state.js, mirrors the player's BUILD 084 field), reset in clearFightScopedState()/startNewRun() alongside the enemy's other fight-scoped fields; once fired, resolveEnemyRoll()'s ENEMY_NAT_ONE branch (pipeline.js, now a real dispatch mirroring resolvePlayerRoll()'s shape, replacing the old stub) logs a plain '[ENEMY ROLL] blank' with no re-trigger. All three new log lines use the existing '[ENEMY]' prefix/colour, no new category. Verified in real windowed Chromium via Playwright, file:// direct, dev-jumped to the boss: forcing faces 5/10/15 each landed 5 player poison stacks with correct decay afterward; forcing enemy Nat 20 fired all three poison faces ascending for 15 total stacks with the log reading exactly as specified; forcing enemy Nat 1 left player HP unchanged after ENEMY_ACT_PHASE, applied 15 self-poison to the enemy, ticked the enemy's own HP down by 15 on the next START_OF_TURN, and forcing face 1 again the same fight produced a plain blank with zero re-poison; Restart Fight reset the once-per-fight flag; normal/elite slots confirmed hasDie: false, unreachable. Zero console/page errors. Build stamp reads STAGE 2.28 / BUILD 097.

Stage 2.27 — a tone pass on the existing sounds (BUILD 096) — landed; no new sounds, events, gameplay, numbers, or visuals — only how the existing sounds sound. Fixed the structural cause first: almost every sound shared the same square-wave, same-instant-attack voice, differing only by frequency, so pitch alone could never make them feel distinct. Register lowered substantially on every frequently-fired sound (roll 1400->620Hz, card_attack 1800->480Hz, damage_enemy 2200->820Hz, mod_trigger 1100->420Hz, end_turn 550->200Hz). Waveform reassigned per the prompt: square/sawtooth reserved for sounds that should feel harsh (Nat 1 unchanged; damage_player kept sawtooth; fight_lost explicitly CHANGED sine->sawtooth, since it was named as one of the three harsh sounds and was the one still soft); everything else moved to sine/triangle. Short fade-ins added to soften former instant clicks (roll, card_attack, card_block, card_hybrid, mod_trigger, damage_enemy, block_absorb, end_turn, fight_won, fight_start_normal/elite, card_reward_basic/rich) — damage_player and fight_lost deliberately kept instant-attack (a hit and a loss should feel abrupt). card_attack/card_block/card_hybrid now differ across waveform, envelope, attack time, duration, and single-tone-vs-interval (hybrid is the only one built from two simultaneous tones) so they read as three different actions, not three notes. block_absorb gained real body (18ms fade, 170ms duration). end_turn lost its old square double-tick entirely, now one quiet 200Hz sine blip at gain 0.045 — the quietest sound in the table, since it fires every turn. Load, Strengthen, Nat 20, and Nat 1 are byte-for-byte unchanged, per the prompt's explicit exemption; fight_start_boss and boss_defeated were reviewed (not named exempt) but left unchanged since neither was ever square, tinny, or frequent. A full written spec (waveform/frequency/envelope/duration per sound) was added as a comment block at the top of audio.js. No SOUND_TABLE entries added/removed/renamed, no announce-site touched in any other file, no change to the two pitch chains or their 8-step cap, no change to mute. Verified in real windowed Chromium via Playwright, file:// direct: SOUND_TABLE key set matches BUILD 095 exactly; Strike/Ward/Rite in one turn fired card_attack/damage_enemy/card_block/card_hybrid/damage_enemy with Chain A offsets 0,1,2 across the card sounds; Nat 20 across 10 loaded faces still produced mod_trigger offsets 0..8,8; muting still silenced oscillator creation while announcing the event; a full fight to a win (5 rounds) produced 47 total sound events with no duplicate or orphaned firings. Zero console/page errors. Build stamp reads STAGE 2.27 / BUILD 096.

Stage 2.26 — the sound chain and five new sounds (BUILD 095) — landed; same rules as BUILD 094 throughout. PART 1: two independent Balatro-style pitch chains, Chain A (cards played) and Chain B (mods triggered), living entirely inside audio.js — playAudioEvent() itself detects a chained event name, passes the current step (capped at 8) as the sound function's argument, and advances the counter afterward, so every game-logic call site is unchanged and unaware chains exist. The step becomes a 2^(steps/12) frequency ratio applied to a chained sound's existing frequencies only — timbre untouched. Both chains reset via a new resetSoundChains(), called directly (not a sound) from runPhase()'s START_OF_TURN. Chain B needed a new mod_trigger event/sound, announced from the existing 'mod_dispatch' MOD_TRIGGER listener (cards-mods.js) — the one funnel both a normal roll and Nat 20's loop already share, so no new detection logic, just a new identity for something with one existing dispatch point. PART 2, five new sounds: fight_start_normal/elite/boss (beginFightFromSlot(), keyed off slot.label; boss is a slow-attack low drone allowed past 200ms "to carry weight," the same exception the Nat sounds get); boss_defeated, a new bigger sound branched inside runPhase()'s win guard so the boss case gets it INSTEAD OF fight_won, fixing boss-kill and normal-kill previously sounding identical; die_action_load (spooky: two 8Hz-detuned sine tones with a slow 40ms fade-in — playTone() gained an optional attackMs argument for this, every existing caller omits it unchanged) and die_action_strengthen (plain, instant, non-detuned) replacing BUILD 094's single shared die_action_confirmed entirely; and card reward split into card_reward_basic (Strike/Ward) / card_reward_rich (everything else) in cardRewardPickCard() — worth recording: gameState.config.cardPool never actually contains 'strike'/'ward' (grep-confirmed), so the basic branch is implemented exactly as specified but currently unreachable in real play; reported rather than worked around, pool contents untouched. Verified in real windowed Chromium via Playwright, file:// direct, with the SOUND_TABLE spy extended to also record each call's argument: five cards in one turn (3 attack/1 block/1 attack) fired with offsets 0,1,2,3,4 exactly, timbre still tracking each card's type, a manual mod trigger in between did not touch the card chain's counter, and the next turn's first card fired at offset 0 (reset confirmed); 10 loaded mods forced through Nat 20 fired mod_trigger 10 times with offsets 0,1,2,...,8,8 — climbing one step per mod and correctly holding at the cap; fight-start fired the correct one of three variants per slot type; boss defeat fired boss_defeated exactly once with no fight_won; Load/Strengthen fired distinct single events each; a real reward pick fired card_reward_rich, and a test-aliased 'strike' pick (temporary monkeypatch, not a production change) fired card_reward_basic; muting silenced oscillator creation while events still announced. A full fight to a win (6 rounds) produced 46 total sound events with no duplicate or orphaned firings. Zero console/page errors. Build stamp reads STAGE 2.26 / BUILD 095.

Stage 2.25 — the sound pass: twelve new synthesised sounds (BUILD 094) — landed; no new systems, no gameplay/number/visual changes. Every sound announces from a place the game logic already knows the event occurred — none invented a new detection point. Card play attack/block/hybrid: playCard() (cards-mods.js), classified by card.type with rite/litany special-cased to hybrid (the only two cards whose effect() calls both dealDamage() and dealBlock()); utility cards get no card-play sound. Damage-landing (enemy and player) share one hook inside dealDamage() (pipeline.js), gated on damage > 0 — poison ticks bypass dealDamage() entirely so they're silent for free. Block-absorb announces in ENEMY_ACT_PHASE (phase-machine.js) from Math.min(intent, block) > 0, placed before dealDamage() so a partially-blocked hit fires block-absorb then damage-landing, each exactly once; fully-blocked fires only block-absorb, unblocked fires only damage-landing. End turn announces from the endTurnBtn click handler. Nat 20/Nat 1 are new dedicated events split out of resolvePlayerRoll()'s rollOutcome branch (previously both shared the generic roll click); a spent Nat 1 still correctly falls through to roll_blank. Fight won/lost announce inside runPhase()'s existing win/loss guard, which already fires exactly once per fight. Die action confirmed announces from both dieActionPickLoadFace()/dieActionPickStrengthenFace() right after their own updateDie() commits. All twelve built on a new shared playTone(waveform, freqStart, freqEnd, durationMs, peakGain) synth in audio.js (playClick() is now a thin wrapper, unchanged behaviour) — distinguishability designed explicitly via waveform/register/sweep-direction/single-vs-multi-click/detuning, documented per sound. Nat 20 (three ascending clicks, ~260ms) and Nat 1 (a 260ms detuned descending sweep) are the only two exceeding 200ms, per the prompt; the other ten are all under 200ms by construction. Mute is checked once inside playTone(), so the existing checkbox silences all twelve with no duplication. No audio files; /audio/ confirmed still empty. Verified in real windowed Chromium via Playwright, file:// direct, using a SOUND_TABLE spy (every entry wrapped to record its name in order) plus an oscillator-creation counter rather than trying to hear anything: Strike fired exactly [card_attack, damage_enemy]; Ward fired [card_block]; Rite fired [card_hybrid, damage_enemy] with no block_absorb; a partially-blocked enemy attack fired [block_absorb, damage_player] in that order once each, a fully-blocked one fired block_absorb alone, an unblocked one fired damage_player alone; forcing face 20 fired only nat_20, forcing face 1 fresh fired only nat_1, forcing face 1 again (spent) fired only roll_blank; forcing enemy/player hp to 0 (via the same runPhase() re-entry the real win/loss paths use) fired fight_won/fight_lost exactly once each; Load and Strengthen each fired die_action_confirmed exactly once; muted, forcing Nat 20's three-click cascade still announced the event but created zero oscillators.
