Stage 2.33 (BUILD 102) — one constants file: js/config.js (new, loaded first — eleven files total) holds GAME_CONFIG plus the FACTS block (F01-F28) verbatim from Notion; every tunable number/structural constant the prompt named now reads from it; tests/facts.test.js (new, 23/23 passing) asserts every fact against GAME_CONFIG and live gameState; F28's wording corrected to the real measured sound-duration exemption set (four sounds beyond the two Nat sounds it previously named).

Stage 2.34 (BUILD 103) — trigger-count indicator: gameState.die.triggerCounts (new, fight-scoped) tracks how many times each face has triggered, bumped once per MOD_TRIGGER (cards-mods.js's mod_dispatch, the single funnel both a normal roll and Nat 20's loop already use); renderDieList() badges every loaded face on the player's own die with ↻N (zero renders nothing), same box/font/size as the ×N weight badge. Closes KI-11.

Stage 2.35 (BUILD 104) — font unification pass: every player-facing text selector that didn't already carry var(--game-font) at .panel-title's own weight (die-mod, face-btn, die-weight, die-trigger-count, stat-label, stat-value) now does. No layout/colour/gameplay change; font-family/font-weight only.

Stage 2.36 (BUILD 105) — tests/autoplay.js: a headless autoplayer, a measurement tool (never ships), plays complete runs against real gameplay only (New Run, map clicks, playCard(), nextPhase()'s own natural rolls, the real die-action/rite/card-reward panels — never a dev forcing tool) under a seeded Math.random and one fixed, in-file-documented policy, writing one CSV row per run to tests/autoplay_results.csv. No gameplay/numbers change. See tests/ list above for the full description.

Stage 2.37 (BUILD 113) — twelfth mod, Anthem: 6 damage plus 4 per point of weight on its own face (weight 1 -> 10, weight 2 -> 14, weight 3 -> 18). Reuses dealDamage() tagged 'attack' (Smite's path — Fervour doubles it identically) and gameState.turn.rolledFaceWeight (Covenant's own read) — no new function, no new listener, no new sound. tests/mods.test.js gained one test (12/12); tests/facts.test.js's F24 updated to 12 (23/23 unchanged). See HISTORY.md for the Playwright verification.

Stage 2.38 (BUILD 114) — thirteenth mod, Elevation: 10 damage; the face directly above the triggering face (faceNumber + 1) permanently gains +1 weight, but only if that face is loaded and is not face 20. Weight write goes through strengthenFace() (pipeline.js), the only place any face's weight is ever written — same as Ordain. No new function, no new listener, no new sound. tests/mods.test.js gained one test (13/13); tests/facts.test.js's F24 updated to 13 (23/23 unchanged). See HISTORY.md for the Playwright verification.

Stage 2.39 (BUILD 115) — multi-mod faces: a face can now hold up to two mods (modId, modId2 — load order), cap two, never three. Both trigger on that face's roll, first-loaded first, each resolving fully before the next; a two-mod face triggers both under Nat 20 too, within its own turn in the ascending sequence. Load offers a second mod onto an already-loaded face only once no blank face remains. Display: both mod names stack in the one die row (new .die-mod-stack), no new row, still twenty rows. Face weight still only ever written by strengthenFace() — untouched by this build. Faces 1/20 untouched. tests/mods.test.js gained three tests (16/16); tests/facts.test.js unchanged (23/23). See HISTORY.md for the Playwright verification.

Stage 2.40 (BUILD 108) — per-mod trigger counts: BUILD 103's separate gameState.die.triggerCounts array (fight-scoped, indexed by face number) folded into each face's own modData — modData.triggerCount for modId, modData.triggerCount2 for modId2 on a two-mod face — matching the carry-forward rule the original array had violated. Now run-scoped, not fight-scoped: survives a fight reset (Restart Fight), wiped only by a brand new run. mod_dispatch (cards-mods.js) matches data.modId against the firing face's modId/modId2 to bump only the mod that actually triggered. Zeal's own effect corrected in the same build to merge into modData instead of replacing it wholesale (it would otherwise have erased a face's trigger count on every Zeal trigger). Display: one-mod face unchanged (↻N); two-mod face shows both counts separated by a slash, first-loaded mod first (↻N1/N2). tests/mods.test.js gained four tests (20/20); tests/facts.test.js unchanged (23/23). See HISTORY.md for the Playwright verification.

Stage 2.41 (BUILD 109) — the run record: a player-facing, localStorage-backed log of every run (see RUN RECORD), built from scratch (no prior copy-run-line button or clipboard/localStorage code existed anywhere — flagged and approved before writing any code). Every Load offer shown/picked, every Skip, per-mod trigger counts (read fresh off face.modData at flush time), node reached, arrival HP at the boss, outcome, per-fight and total round counts, and a source column ('human'/'bot') — written as it happens, flushed on win/loss/New-Run-mid-run/tab-close. New #copyRunRecordBtn dumps everything stored to the clipboard. Build stamp corrected to BUILD 109 (was stuck at BUILD 104). tests/facts.test.js gained five tests (28/28); tests/mods.test.js unchanged (20/20). See HISTORY.md for the Playwright verification.

Stage 2.42 (BUILD 116) — three fixes: the trigger-count badge's '↻N' glyph replaced with a legible '#N' label at a bigger font size (14px -> 17px, with an explicit line-height fix for a 1px row-growth bug that font bump exposed); weight made visible on the face itself via a currentColor fill inside face-btn, scaled to weight (no new colour — see DIE FACE OBJECT STRUCTURE's weight display note); and BUILD 115's "second mod only once no blanks remain" Load gate removed entirely — any loaded, non-Nat, not-yet-full face is now a valid Load target at any point in a run (see MULTI-MOD FACES). tests/facts.test.js gained three tests (31/31); tests/mods.test.js unchanged (20/20). See HISTORY.md for the Playwright verification.

Stage 2.43 (BUILD 117) — three die-row fixes plus one investigation: two mods now render side by side on one line, each with its own trigger count beside it (replaces BUILD 115's vertical stack, which broke row height — a genuine layout bug found and fixed this build, not assumed away); the weight fill is now visible starting at weight 2 (raised floor + opacity — weight 2 previously looked identical to weight 1 in real play); anthem and elevation both gained the MOD_DESCRIPTION hover entries they'd been missing since their own builds (checked, not just Elevation, the one symptom reported — anthem had the identical gap). The run-record dieActionEvents gap was investigated and could not be reproduced in three realistic scenarios; no fix applied since no defect was found. Build stamp corrected to BUILD 117, and CLAUDE.md gained a standing instruction (WHO EDITS THIS FILE) that every build updates it going forward. tests/facts.test.js gained three tests (34/34); tests/mods.test.js unchanged (20/20). See HISTORY.md for the Playwright verification.

Stage 2.44 (BUILD 118) — KI-19: collectTriggerCountsByMod() (run-and-map.js) OVERWROTE a mod's trigger count with whichever face was processed last instead of summing across every face carrying it, silently discarding every earlier face's count — invisible through the real Load flow (which can never put the same mod on two faces) but real once a die was built through devLoadMod(), exactly how two real runs recorded consecrate:1/blight:0 against dice where those mods sat on many faces with real nonzero counts. Now sums correctly across faces and both slots, and seeds every real mod at 0 so one never loaded this run reports 0 instead of being omitted. New dev tool, Load All (#devLoadAllBtn): fills every available slot on the player die with whichever mod is selected in the existing dev-load dropdown — a stress tool built specifically to reach the "same mod on many faces" shape this bug needed to surface, and to make it trivial to build again for any future test. tests/facts.test.js gained three tests (37/37); tests/mods.test.js unchanged (20/20). See HISTORY.md for the Playwright verification.

Stage 2.45 (BUILD 119) — measurement session, not a feature build: re-measured the bot win rate D-08 recorded at 34% at BUILD 105. No design/policy change to the bot (resolveDieAction()/LOAD_PRIORITY untouched); two run-record plumbing additions to tests/autoplay.js only — source:'bot' tagging via updateRunRecord() right after #startGameBtn's click, and playRun() now returns its own run's localStorage-captured run-record line(s) (needed once browser.newPage()'s per-call context isolation was discovered this build to prevent localStorage accumulating across a batch's runs the way a human's persistent profile would). 400 seeded games (seeds 1-400, asBuilt, riteHeal 20): win rate 29.75% (119/400), down from 105's 34%. Second-slot usage and Strengthen-20 usage both measured at 0, for structural reasons stated in full in CURRENT SUBSTAGE, not because run-record coverage missed them. tests/facts.test.js and tests/mods.test.js both unchanged (37/37, 20/20). New git-trackable artifact: run_record_master.csv (404 lines incl. header) in the project root, per D-40. See CURRENT SUBSTAGE for the full measurement and the Playwright verification.

Stage 2.46 (BUILD 112) — "make verification honest": closes KI-14 (facts.test.js's circular comment-vs-GAME_CONFIG assertions now labelled SPEC-ONLY, 18 assertion-sites/14 tests, real behavioural assertions untouched), KI-9 (checked the resetFight()/clearListeners('turn') gap empirically — real for a moment, never reaches actual gameplay), and KI-2 (one-line run.status guard on playCard()); builds the registry inspector from REG-E2. tests/facts.test.js gained two tests (39/39, up from 37/37); tests/mods.test.js unchanged (20/20). Found and reported, not fixed: BUILD 107 has no CLAUDE.md index line either, and "Stage 2.37" is used by both BUILD 107 and BUILD 113. See HISTORY.md for the full write-up and the Playwright verification. (This index line itself was missed when BUILD 112 landed — added retroactively by BUILD 120, which found the gap.)

Stage 2.47 (BUILD 120) — the screenshot baseline KI-6 had queued: tests/screenshots.js (new) drives to five screens (map, a fight with a two-mod face and a strengthened face visible, the die action panel, the card reward panel, the dev drawer open) and saves one PNG each into verify/ (new, git-trackable), overwriting every run, on the same chromium.launch()/browser.newPage()/page.goto(FILE_URL) path every other test file already uses. `--compare` mode rotates the previous verify/ set into verify_prev/ and reports a per-screen changed-pixel count via tests/pngdiff.js (new) — a hand-rolled PNG decoder over Node's built-in zlib, no new dependency added. First baseline generated and confirmed real (two files opened and visually inspected); a real bug was caught and fixed while building it — see CURRENT SUBSTAGE. New standing instruction (WHO EDITS THIS FILE): every build regenerates verify/ and reports the diff in its paste-back. tests/facts.test.js and tests/mods.test.js both unchanged (39/39, 20/20) before and after — no js/ file was touched. See CURRENT SUBSTAGE for the full build and the caught-bug detail.

Stage 2.48 (BUILD 121) — D-53 sweep: every bare use of "stacks" (a stacking-status count with no status named beside it) in a log string or a GAME_CONFIG comment now names the status it counts. Three log-string fixes, all in the poison path only (poison is the only stacking status built, per D-53) — cards-mods.js's enemy-buff-on-player line and its Nat-1 self-poison line, both changed from "...stacks now N" to "...N stacks of poison to X, now N stacks of poison"; phase-machine.js's enemy poison-tick line changed from "damage, stacks now N" to "damage, N stacks of poison remaining". config.js's mirrored FACTS comments (F19, F23) reworded the same way, synced to 1 State §2.1 in the same build. MOD_DESCRIPTION (rendering.js) audited — no bare "stacks" found there; none of the thirteen mod descriptions use the word. Variable names (poisonStacks, GAME_CONFIG.*_STACKS, etc.) untouched, per the prompt's explicit instruction — this is wording only, no gameplay change. tests/facts.test.js and tests/mods.test.js both unchanged (39/39, 20/20) before and after. See CURRENT SUBSTAGE for the Playwright-verified log line.

Stage 2.49 (BUILD 122) — KI-18, the stale build stamp: GAME_CONFIG.BUILD (config.js, new) is now the single source of truth for the on-screen build number. index.html's #buildStamp keeps its own static "STAGE X.Y" text but the number after "BUILD" is a new empty `<span id="buildStampNumber">`, written once at init() (cards-mods.js, right before the existing "[INIT] gameState initialised" log line) from GAME_CONFIG.BUILD — no second hand-typed copy of the number left anywhere in index.html. The standing CLAUDE.md instruction alone (added BUILD 117) had already failed twice (BUILD 116, BUILD 118 — KI-18), so the fix this time is a test, not a third instruction: tests/facts.test.js gained one new test that reads GAME_CONFIG.BUILD from config.js and independently reads CLAUDE.md's own CONFIRMED WORKING section, extracts every "(BUILD nnn)" entry it names, and asserts the highest one equals GAME_CONFIG.BUILD — plus a live-page check that the rendered #buildStampNumber DOM text matches. A future build that bumps GAME_CONFIG.BUILD without adding its own CONFIRMED WORKING line (or vice versa) now fails this test immediately instead of being noticed cold several builds later. tests/facts.test.js gained one test (40/40); tests/mods.test.js unchanged (20/20). FOUND, NOT FIXED: this build never wrote a CURRENT SUBSTAGE section — see BUILD 123's CURRENT SUBSTAGE entry.

Stage 2.50 (BUILD 123) — checkpoint 1 on 8 North star, OQ-13: LOAD_PRIORITY (tests/autoplay.js) replaced with the twelve loadable mods in Fergus's own rank order from 1 State §6 (Fervour 1 through Vigil 12; Consecrate is the anchor, never ranked, never offered) — queried directly against the live Notion mod-pool database and confirmed to match byte-for-byte. Replaces the BUILD 105 hand-tuned 9-mod list (missing Virulence, Anthem, Elevation entirely, and special-casing Virulence-after-Blight) with a flat rank walk — nothing left to decide, per the checkpoint's own framing. New test, tests/facts.test.js: "BUILD 123: LOAD_PRIORITY ranks every loadable mod exactly once" — reads the live page's gameState.config.mods, asserts LOAD_PRIORITY has no duplicates and matches the loadable set exactly; tests/facts.test.js gained one test (41/41); tests/mods.test.js unchanged (20/20). Re-ran the single-combo 400-run seeded baseline (seeds 1-400, asBuilt, riteHeal 20) that D-08's 34% and BUILD 119's 29.75% both rest on: **win rate 25.8% (103/400)**, median arrival HP 38, median faces at boss 7, median final round 6 — this is now the number D-08 should read, down from both prior measurements. See CURRENT SUBSTAGE for the caught invocation bug and two found-not-fixed documentation gaps.

Stage 2.51 (BUILD 124) — closes the CSV stale-build-stamp bug BUILD 123 flagged: tests/autoplay.js's own `const BUILD = 105` (never bumped since the file was written) used to stamp every CSV row's `build` column regardless of which build actually produced the run; removed outright, and the column is now read live from GAME_CONFIG.BUILD per run via `page.evaluate()`. tests/facts.test.js gained one new test ("BUILD 124: autoplay.js has no own BUILD constant and stamps its CSV from GAME_CONFIG.BUILD") that asserts the source has no such constant and that a real one-run batch's CSV row carries GAME_CONFIG.BUILD; tests/facts.test.js gained one test (42/42); tests/mods.test.js unchanged (20/20). Existing tests/autoplay_results.csv rows (1,606 total, all bot-sourced) relabelled by provable build boundary, never guessed: rows 1-402 → BUILD 105, 403-804 → BUILD 119, 805-1606 → BUILD 123, established from backups/ snapshot row-counts at four timestamps cross-checked against each build's own documented batch composition (BUILD 105's 2 reproducibility rows + 400-run 4-combination sweep; BUILD 119's 2 reproducibility rows + single-combination 400-run baseline; BUILD 123's caught-bug 402-row default-protocol misfire + its corrected 400-run baseline) — content (dieRewardRule/riteHeal/seed pattern) matched exactly at every claimed boundary, not inferred from timestamps alone. D-40's own run_record_master.csv has no build column at all (RUN_RECORD_CSV_HEADER, run-and-map.js) and was not touched. See CURRENT SUBSTAGE for the full evidence trail and the paste-back detail.

Stage 2.52 (BUILD 125) — checkpoint 2, the three-act run (F31/F32): buildAct(actNumber) now bakes per-act enemy scaling (GAME_CONFIG.ACT_HP_MULTIPLIER/ACT_INTENT_MULTIPLIER, [1.0,1.4,1.9]/[1.0,1.2,1.45], Math.ceil'd at act-build time) into every enemy's hp/intentMin/intentMax and the enemy buff's own poison amount (gameState.enemy.buffPoisonStacks, 5/6/8 across acts 1/2/3); the enemy Nat 1 self-poison stays flat, unscaled. Beating the act 1 or act 2 boss now grants a die + card reward exactly like any fight win, then starts the next act (advanceRun()'s new 'boss' branch — same currentSlot:'opening'/lane:null startNewRun() itself sets, no heal, per D-27); only the act 3 boss is VICTORY, no reward (D-22, unchanged there). Act number shown on both the map screen title and a new #actStamp on the fight screen. tests/facts.test.js gained two new tests (44/44); tests/mods.test.js unchanged (20/20). FOUND, NOT FIXED: the build stamp's hand-typed "STAGE X.Y" text stayed at 2.51 this build (the GAME_CONFIG.BUILD-driven number itself was correct) — caught and fixed at BUILD 126. See ACTS (new standing section) for the full mechanic and HISTORY.md for the verification.

Stage 2.53 (BUILD 126) — pool exhaustion stopgap (D-54): dieActionChooseLoad() now converts to Strengthen the instant fewer than three unloaded, non-anchor mods remain in the pool, before dieActionMods/dieActionStep are ever touched — a real 3-mod Load offer can no longer be built at that point, so this is what keeps that offer from ever coming up short or repeating a mod. tests/facts.test.js gained one new test (45/45); tests/mods.test.js unchanged (20/20). Also closes BUILD 125's own build-stamp gap (index.html's stage text was stuck at 2.51; now 2.53, covering both the missed bump and this build's own). REVISED AT BUILD 127: this click-time redirect was a real behaviour fix but not a UI fix — a player still saw a clickable "Load" button that silently did something else, and the bot (which calls functions directly, never DOM buttons) had no way to know the redirect had fired, causing a real crash during BUILD 127's three-act baseline. The Load button is now hidden outright at the 'choose' step once the pool is this low (renderDieActionPanel(), gated on the same new eligibleLoadModIds() count); dieActionChooseLoad() itself keeps the redirect as a defensive fallback for a direct call, but the button is the real fix. See BUILD 127's CONFIRMED WORKING and CURRENT SUBSTAGE.

Stage 2.54 (BUILD 127) — the bot plays all three acts (checkpoint 2 closes): tests/autoplay.js's playRun() now loops every act via a new playOneAct() helper instead of stopping at one boss; tests/autoplay_results.csv gained an actReached column (existing rows blanked, never guessed). Found and fixed mid-build: the bot crashed once a real 400-run baseline actually reached pool exhaustion (never possible in a single-act run) — traced to D-54's BUILD 126 fix being a click-time redirect a DOM-clicking player would never trigger by accident but a function-calling bot could reach in a state it didn't check for; fixed by hiding the Load button outright (see BUILD 126's amended entry) and updating the bot's own policy and the D-54 test to match. Test suites unchanged (20/20 mods, 45/45 facts — the D-54 test rewritten, not added). A 20-run smoke check confirmed three-act runs complete cleanly post-fix; the full 400-run baseline was not run this session, on explicit instruction mid-build. See CURRENT SUBSTAGE for the full account.

Stage 2.55 (BUILD 128) — checkpoint 3 map: each lane grows from five slots to eight (F16), so every act now has 7 fights (opening + 5 lane fights/elite + boss), 21 a run. buildAct() (run-and-map.js) keeps each lane's original five slots as an exact prefix (byte-identical shapes to the pre-128 build) and appends three more (Fight, Fight, Rite) — upper: Fight, Rite, Fight, Elite, Rite, Fight, Fight, Rite; lower: Fight, Rite, Fight, Fight, Rite, Fight, Fight, Rite. A normal fight's enemy "type" (which of the three NORMAL_FIGHT_HP entries it uses) is a per-lane 0/1/2 rotation over slot order (rites/the elite don't advance it), which by construction never repeats on the very next normal fight in that lane; each normal fight's enemy now carries its own normalTypeIndex for this to be asserted directly. Rewards (a card after every non-final-boss win; die rewards fight 1/elite 2/rite 1/boss 1 per non-final act, none on the final boss) needed no change — phase-machine.js's win branch already keys off wonSlot.label/currentSlot==='boss', not a slot index, so it generalises to the extra slots automatically. GAME_CONFIG.SLOTS_PER_LANE is now 8; ELITE_ALLOWED_SLOT_INDICES narrowed to [3] (F16 now names one slot, not a range); new RITE_SLOT_INDICES [1,4,7]. KI-24 (D-29): refreshInspector()'s enemy panel no longer hides the die column outright for a dieless (hasDie: false) enemy — it now renders the column with an empty die slot (renderDieList() also fixed to clear a container's stale rows when handed zero faces, not just early-return), the same code path for every act, closing the gap for acts 1-3 alike. KI-25 (D-53): the two remaining bare "N poison" log lines (cards-mods.js's blight and virulence mod effects) now read "N stacks of poison" ("...doubled to N stacks of poison" for virulence), matching every other poison log line's BUILD 121 wording. tests/autoplay.js's playOneAct() lane traversal is now read live off gameState.run.act.upper's own length/slot types instead of a hardcoded 5-slot array, so the same bot policy plays both the old and new lane shapes with no code change; it also now tracks every completed fight's own round count (not just each run's last) and each act's own boss-arrival HP, so runBatch()'s summary reports mean rounds/fight and per-act mean HP entering that boss. tests/facts.test.js gained three new tests (F16 rewritten for the 8-slot shape, a no-repeat-adjacent-normal-enemy assertion, a 7-fights-per-path assertion) plus the KI-25 log-wording assertion (48/48); tests/mods.test.js unchanged (20/20). 200-run seeded bot baselines (seed 1-200, asBuilt, riteHeal 20) before/after: see this build's paste-back for the full before/after table (win rate, mean rounds/fight, mean HP entering each act's boss).

Stage 2.56 (BUILD 129) — checkpoint 3 tiers: every offerable mod and card now carries a tier field ('common'/'uncommon'/'rare', gameState.config.mods/cardPool) — mods: common Smite/Sanctuary/Penance/Blight/Offering/Anthem, uncommon Vigil/Ordain/Zeal/Elevation, rare Virulence/Fervour; cards: common Rebuke/Censure/Vestment/Litany/Scripture/Interdict/Orison/Censer/Purge, uncommon Judgement/Reckoning/Communion/Rapture/Covenant/Retribution. Consecrate and the three starters (Strike/Ward/Rite) carry no tier field and remain unofferable, unchanged. Card rewards (openCardRewardScreen()) and Load offers (dieActionChooseLoad()) now each roll a tier per choice, independently, off a per-slot [common,uncommon,rare] split (GAME_CONFIG.TIER_SPLIT) keyed by which slot the reward/offer came from (rendering.js's new currentOfferTierSplit()): fight/rite 65/30/5, elite 40/40/20, boss 0/70/30 — a rite's Load offer (dieActionOrigin === 'rite') shares the fight split. New shared picker (state.js's rollTier()/pickFromTier()/pickTieredOffer()) walks GAME_CONFIG.TIER_ORDER down (toward common) then up (toward rare) whenever a rolled tier's pool is empty, and never repeats a piece within one offer. D-54's Load-visibility rule changed: the Load button now shows whenever a blank face exists among faces 2-19 (renderDieActionPanel()), with the existing fewer-than-3-eligible-mods gate kept only as a last guard underneath it (dieActionChooseLoad()'s own defensive Strengthen-conversion fallback is unchanged). tests/facts.test.js gained five new tests — every mod's tier, every card's tier, Consecrate/Strike/Ward/Rite never offered (500 samples each of Load and card reward), a seeded 10,000-choice fight-split sample within 2 points of 65/30/5 (fallbacks tallied separately, off the rolled tier not the delivered piece's tier), and a 2000-sample x 3-splits x 2-pools no-repeat check — 48/48 -> 53/53; tests/mods.test.js unchanged (20/20). See CURRENT SUBSTAGE for the full write-up.

Stage 2.58 (BUILD 131) — checkpoint 3, sixteen new cards needing no new engine code: card count 15 -> 31 (F25 updated in config.js's header comment and tests/facts.test.js's own assertion — CLAUDE.md itself carries no separate copy of the FACTS text, only config.js's header and the Notion "Die — V1" page do, per PROJECT's own description of config.js; the Notion page was not reachable this session and was not updated — flagged, not silently skipped). All sixteen added to gameState.config.cards and referenced (not copied) into config.cardPool exactly like every existing reward-pool card: Tenet (uncommon, Growth+Mass — 6 damage +3 per the rolled face's own modData.triggerCount, run-scoped per BUILD 108, cap 24; a blank rolled face carries no modData so the read falls through to 0, the same fallthrough Zeal/Cope's own bonus reads already use); Gradual (uncommon, Mass — 3 damage +3 per the heaviest loaded face's weight, cap 12); Vacancy (common, Mass — 1 damage per blank face among faces 2-19, cap 16); Lauds (common, Growth — 4 damage +3 per loaded mod carrying the Growth tag across both slots, two mods on one face counting twice, cap 13, same modId/modId2 tag scan Congregation already uses); Chastise (common, no tags — 7 damage); Cloister (common, no tags — 7 block); Psalm (common, no tags — draws 1 through the same drawCards() path Scripture already uses); Reliquary (common, Bastion — 6 block, plus 5 damage if block was 10 or more before playing it, block read before Reliquary's own dealBlock() call raises it); Vindication (rare, Bastion — damage equal to twice block, cap 24, block read only, never spent, same shape Retribution/Anathema already use); Myrrh (uncommon, Poison — 6 block +1 per stack of poison on the enemy, cap 12); Exequy (rare, Poison — damage equal to the enemy's stacks of poison, cap 12, stacks not removed); Hosanna (common, Soul — 6 damage, 11 if soul is still 3 or more after playCard()'s own cost deduction, which runs before a card's effect(), so no separate "after paying" read was needed); Gloria (uncommon, Soul — 30 damage, cost 4, no special-case code: renderCardButtons()'s existing soul>=cost check already dims it on base soul 3 like any other card); Oblation (rare, Soul — spends all soul via the same updatePlayer({soul:...}) path Communion/Largesse already use, 7 damage per soul spent, cap 42, soul becomes 0); Tabernacle (common, Mass — 3 block +3 per the rolled face's own weight, reading gameState.turn.rolledFaceWeight exactly as Covenant already does, cap 12); Jubilee (rare, Growth+Mass — 4 damage +2 per weight added to the die this run, cap 24, weight added being the sum of all 20 faces' own weight fields minus 20, since every face starts at weight 1). tests/facts.test.js gained eighteen new tests — one per card (two each for Tenet, Oblation and the Gloria/Reliquary/Vacancy/Lauds/Hosanna condition-plus-cap pairs folded into their own single tests) — each playing the real card through playCard() (never card.effect() directly), picking inputs that push the raw value past the cap so the cap is actually exercised, plus the reward-pool tier and tags deepStrictEqual maps extended for all sixteen — 63/63 -> 81/81; tests/mods.test.js unchanged (26/26). GAME_CONFIG.BUILD bumped 130 -> 131; index.html's stage text corrected to 2.58 in the same build (WHO EDITS THIS FILE / KI-18).

Stage 2.57 (BUILD 130) — checkpoint 3 tags, plus six new mods needing no other new engine code: every mod and card now carries a `tags` field (a list of words, several allowed per piece) — Poison: Blight/Virulence/Censer/Purge/Reckoning; Mass: Fervour/Elevation/Zeal/Ordain/Anthem/Covenant; Growth: Zeal/Elevation/Ordain; Soul: Consecrate/Offering/Communion/Rapture; Bastion: Sanctuary/Penance/Vigil/Retribution; everything else (including the three starters) gets `[]`. Six new mods, mod count 13 -> 19 (F24 updated in config.js's header comment, CLAUDE.md's FACTS, and tests/facts.test.js's assertion, all three): Largesse (common, Soul — +2 soul, 4 block); Tithe (uncommon, Soul — end of round, 5 damage per soul remaining, capped 20, registered on the same generic phase-name hook Vigil already uses); Congregation (uncommon, Growth — 8 damage, 16 if another loaded mod carries the Growth tag, never counts itself, which falls out for free since eligibleLoadModIds() already forbids loading the same mod twice); Cope (common, Growth+Bastion — 8 block, permanently +2 per trigger, stored in the triggering face's own modData exactly the way Zeal already stores its +4, ARCH-CF2); Anathema (uncommon, Bastion — end of round, damage equal to current block capped 16, block read not spent, same END_PLAYER_TURN-equivalent hook as Tithe/Vigil); Thurible (common, Poison — 8 damage, applies 3 stacks of poison, log line reads "stacks of poison" from the start, never bare). All six added to config.mods (offerable through the existing tier/Load machinery unchanged) and appended to the end of tests/autoplay.js's own LOAD_PRIORITY list (never run this session, per instruction). tests/mods.test.js gained six new per-mod tests (20/20 -> 26/26); tests/facts.test.js gained ten new tests — mod tags, card tags, the three starters' empty tags, one behavioural test per new mod (numbers and cap), and a Load-offerability spot-check (ARCH-CF6) — plus updated its F24/D-54/collectTriggerCountsByMod/MOD_DESCRIPTION-coverage assertions for 19 mods (53/53 -> 63/63). See CURRENT SUBSTAGE for the full write-up.

Stage 2.59 (BUILD 132) — checkpoint 3, trigger a face outside a roll (prompt D): one shared function, triggerFaceOutsideRoll() (pipeline.js) — refuses face 1/20, refuses a face already triggered this way once this round (gameState.turn.outsideTriggeredFaces, cleared at START_OF_TURN), and refuses once GAME_CONFIG.ROUND_TRIGGER_CAP (D-51, new, 10) is reached — used by three new pieces: Threnody (card, uncommon, cost 2, Growth — the lowest-numbered loaded face triggers), Reverberation (card, rare, cost 2, Mass — the rolled face triggers again, or 6 block on a Nat roll), Magnificat (mod, rare, Mass — the heaviest other loaded face triggers, ties to the lowest number, own face never counts). Every real MOD_TRIGGER dispatch counts toward the round cap except a Nat 20 sweep, tagged natTwentySweep to exempt it. Mod count 19 -> 20, reward card count 31 -> 33 (F24/F25 updated in config.js's header comment and tests/facts.test.js's assertions). tests/mods.test.js gained four new tests (Magnificat plus three engine-level triggerFaceOutsideRoll() tests: refuses 1/20, refuses a repeat trigger, stops at the cap) — 26/26 -> 30/30; tests/facts.test.js gained six new tests (Threnody, Reverberation x3, a Magnificat Load-offerability spot-check, plus the count/tier/tags map updates) — 81/81 -> 86/86. See CURRENT SUBSTAGE for the full write-up.

Stage 2.60 (BUILD 133) — checkpoint 3, the Bound engine: a die-wide Bound scan (runBoundScan(), pipeline.js), a fight-scoped Bound setter (grantBoundToFace()), a fast sweep (playSweep() — Nat 20 sweeps and Bound scans both speed up after the round's third trigger, timing only), and three plain Bound mods (Unison 6 damage, Accord 10 block, Kinship 4 stacks of poison — all common, tags Bound). Mod count 20 -> 23 (F24 updated in config.js's header comment and tests/facts.test.js's assertions). tests/mods.test.js gained seven new tests (Unison, Accord, Kinship, two loaded Bound faces firing together, the scan not running during a Nat 20 sweep, the setter's refusals, and grants clearing at fight end while unrelated modData survives) — 30/30 -> 37/37; tests/facts.test.js unchanged in count (86/86), updated in place for the new mod total. See CURRENT SUBSTAGE for the full write-up.

Stage 2.61 (BUILD 134) — checkpoint 3, the remaining Bound pieces, closing checkpoint 3: Kyrie (card, common, cost 1, tags Bound — 5 damage, 10 if the rolled face has Bound, via isBoundFace()); Novena (card, rare, cost 2, tags Bound — every loaded Bound face triggers, each through triggerFaceOutsideRoll(), paced by playSweep() the same way runBoundScan() already is); Canticle (card, uncommon, cost 1, tags Bound — 6 block; if the rolled face is loaded, it gains Bound for this fight via grantBoundToFace(), never face 1 or 20); Concord (mod, uncommon, tags Bound+Soul — +1 soul, 3 block, same shape as Largesse's own soul+block combo); Herald (mod, rare, tags Bound — 6 damage; one other random loaded face without Bound gains Bound for the fight, through grantBoundToFace() and a new pickRandom() (state.js) off the same Math.random() source shuffle() uses; no eligible face is a no-op). Mod count 23 -> 25 (24 offerable plus Consecrate: 12 common, 8 uncommon, 4 rare); reward card count 33 -> 36 (18 common, 12 uncommon, 6 rare) — F24/F25 updated in config.js's header comment and tests/facts.test.js's assertions, plus a new explicit tier-total test. Kyrie/Novena/Canticle added to config.cardPool; Concord/Herald added to config.mods (offerable through the existing tier/Load machinery unchanged) and appended to the end of tests/autoplay.js's own LOAD_PRIORITY list (never run this session, per instruction). tests/mods.test.js gained two new tests (Concord; Herald, with Math.random replaced by a fixed sequence to prove its grant lands on a loaded, non-Bound, non-own face and clears at fight end) — 37/37 -> 39/39; tests/facts.test.js gained five new tests (Kyrie x2, Novena, Canticle, the tier-total check) plus updated F24/F25/D-54/collectTriggerCountsByMod/MOD_DESCRIPTION-coverage assertions and both tier/tags maps — 86/86 -> 91/91. See CURRENT SUBSTAGE for the full write-up.

Stage 2.62 (BUILD 135) — win the fight the moment the enemy dies. A card kill already ended the fight without End Turn (BUILD 053/068's own top-of-runPhase() win/loss guard, re-entered on demand) — the gap was BUILD 133's playSweep(): a Nat 20 sweep's or a Bound scan's dispatches are staggered via setTimeout, so a kill from one of those could sit unnoticed until the next real phase transition saw enemy.hp <= 0 cold. New shared checkWinNow() (phase-machine.js) re-enters that same guard on demand; playSweep() (pipeline.js) now takes an optional onComplete, called once every dispatch it queued has actually run (not merely scheduled) — checkWinNow() is passed as that onComplete from runBoundScan(), onNatTwenty() (cards-mods.js), and Novena's own sweep (cards-mods.js), so a kill partway through a sweep still lets its remaining triggers land (permanent growth from them still counts) before the win registers. cards-mods.js's existing card-kill check (playCard()) now calls the same shared checkWinNow() instead of its own inline copy — no behaviour change there, one fewer duplicate. Enemy HP display (rendering.js's renderStats()) now clamps to 0 (`Math.max(0, gameState.enemy.hp)`) — internal state is still allowed to go negative (overkill damage is applied raw, unclamped, exactly as before), only the on-screen text changed. tests/mods.test.js gained one new test (a Nat 20 kill from the sweep registers run.status === 'win' while turn.phase is still ROLL_PHASE, never having reached CARD_PHASE) — 39/39 -> 40/40; tests/facts.test.js gained two new tests (a card kill likewise wins with turn.phase still CARD_PHASE, no nextPhase() call made; the enemy HP display reads "0 / maxHp" off a forced -37 internal hp) — 91/91 -> 93/93. See CURRENT SUBSTAGE for the full write-up, the pre-change findings, and the screenshot diff.

Stage 2.63 (BUILD 136) — on-screen text for every checkpoint 3 piece, plus New Run working while a fight-won panel is open. Twenty-one new cards (Tenet through Canticle) had never been added to CARD_EFFECT_TEXT (rendering.js) — they rendered with no hand text and no reward hover text since the build that added each one — now all twenty-one are entered, in the project's existing style, exact numbers taken from each card's own effect()/log() line. Largesse's MOD_DESCRIPTION read "+2 soul this turn, 4 block" though its effect (updatePlayer(), no turn-scoped clear) is a permanent gain — a real meaning mismatch, fixed to "+2 soul, 4 block"; the other eleven checkpoint-3 mods already matched their own effects in meaning and were left as worded. Every mod carrying the 'growth' tag (zeal, ordain, elevation, congregation, cope) now appends ". Growth" to its MOD_DESCRIPTION, the same suffix convention Bound already used, so a player can see what Lauds'/Congregation's own "loaded Growth mod" count actually counts. New Run (#startGameBtn) used to be flatly disabled whenever dieActionStep/cardRewardStep/riteStep was non-null (refreshInspector(), rendering.js) — a line traced back through BUILD 068's own paste-back to before BUILD 050 (the very first card reward screen), so older than BUILD 129 by roughly eighty builds. That disable is now removed outright, and the click handler (bootstrap.js) resets all three flow variables first, the same dismiss-the-panel fix BUILD 078 already gave devJumpToSlot() — so New Run now both works and actually clears whatever panel was open, rather than becoming clickable but leaving a stale panel rendered. tests/facts.test.js gained six new tests (every reward-pool card has a CARD_EFFECT_TEXT entry, every offerable mod has a MOD_DESCRIPTION entry, a new card renders its hand text, a new card's reward hover is non-empty, New Run works and dismisses an open die-action panel, New Run works and dismisses an open card-reward panel) — 93/93 -> 99/99; tests/mods.test.js unchanged (40/40). See HISTORY.md for the full pre-change findings and the screenshot diff.

Stage 2.64 (BUILD 137) — playtest readiness: weaker enemy poison, a Bound badge, readable two-mod names, a quieter log. Enemy poison buff (ENEMY_BUFF_POISON_STACKS, config.js) lowered from 5 to 3 — act scaling (buildAct(), unchanged) now yields 3/4/5 across acts 1/2/3, was 5/6/8; ENEMY_NAT_ONE_SELF_POISON (a separate knob) untouched. Every Bound face — printed (a mod's own 'bound' tag) or granted for the fight (grantBoundToFace()) — now shows a small "Bound" badge on its die row (renderDieList(), `.die-bound-badge`, same box/font as the existing `.die-weight` ×N badge, D-28's no-new-palette rule), appearing/disappearing exactly when isBoundFace() would flip. A two-mod face's row now shows each name's first 6 letters (TWO_MOD_NAME_CHARS, rendering.js) with no ellipsis, full name via a native title tooltip — replaces BUILD 117's fixed 44px CSS ellipsis, which could cut a name to 2-3 letters plus "…" ("Her…"/"San…"); 6 was measured (Playwright canvas measureText, worst-case pairing) to fit the narrowest die list (#dieActionDieList) at the game's default 1600×1080 window with room to spare, so the 5-letter fallback was not needed. The round-trigger-cap log line (triggerFaceOutsideRoll(), pipeline.js) now prints at most once per round (gameState.turn.roundTriggerCapLogged, cleared at START_OF_TURN) instead of once per refusal. tests/facts.test.js gained four new tests (a printed-Bound face shows the badge; a granted face shows it and loses it at fight end via resetFight(), while the mod itself survives; a two-mod face's row shows both truncated names with no ellipsis and full names via title; the cap line appears once in a round that hits the cap eighteen times) plus F19/F22/F31 rewritten in place for the new poison numbers — 99/99 -> 103/103; tests/mods.test.js unchanged (40/40). See HISTORY.md for the full write-up and the screenshot diff.

Stage 2.65 (BUILD 138) — die feedback: every face that triggers is now SEEN to trigger, and faces 1/20 count their own rolls. A face that fires without being the rolled face (a Nat 20 sweep, a Bound scan, or an outside-roll trigger — Threnody/Reverberation/Magnificat/Novena) now hops to the exact rolled-face look (indent + yellow, the same `.die-row-rolled`/`-flash` classes the BUILD 090 highlight already uses — no new colour, no new indent) at the moment it fires, in firing order, paced by the existing sweep delay, and stays in that look for the rest of the round exactly as a rolled face does — see the new THE HOP standing section. ## Stage 2.71 (BUILD 144)

Stage 2.71 (BUILD 144) — skin pass, no number, mechanic or layout change.

Fonts: two self-hosted OFL font files added under fonts/ (PressStart2P-Regular.ttf, VT323-Regular.ttf, both from the google/fonts repo), declared via two new @font-face rules. --game-font is now 'Press Start 2P' (everywhere it already applied); a new --game-font-2 is 'VT323', used by the body (17px default), #log and #buildStamp. No var(--game-font) size needed reducing — a Playwright overflow scan (scrollWidth/scrollHeight vs clientWidth/clientHeight) across the map, fight, die action, card reward and dev drawer screens found nothing above its box. A two-mod die row's line-box height did grow under the new fonts (VT323's own metrics, inherited by the unstyled `.die-mod-pair` wrapper); fixed with an explicit font-family/font-size/line-height/height on that one class, not a layout change.

Palette: --bg/--panel-bg/--enemy-panel-bg/--player-panel-bg to #000000, --text to #e8e4d0, --muted to #6b6b6b, --line/--enemy-border/--player-border to #2a2a2a; --nat/--player-mod/--enemy-mod/--blank/--phase-color untouched. Every button now black background, 2px solid border, no border-radius; #endTurnBtn is the one filled button (var(--text) fill, black text); #startGameBtn's border/text is var(--enemy-mod). #enemyHpValue/#playerHpValue render in var(--enemy-mod), #playerBlockValue in var(--player-mod), stat labels in --text.

Log toggle: gameState.ui (new, { logOpen: false }) plus updateUi() alongside the other five state helpers. #logToggleBtn (top bar, right of Copy Run Record) flips it; .app is one column with #log hidden when closed, 1fr 380px with #log shown when open. Default closed on every fresh page load; holds across the map, a fight, and New Run within the same page load.

Phase badge: #phaseBadge now shows the phase name with underscores replaced by spaces (CARD PHASE, not CARD_PHASE); the CSS class name driving its colour is unchanged.

Motion: every eased/linear transition and animation timing function in index.html is now steps(2) or steps(3); every duration is unchanged from before this build.

Also: every .face-btn and hand card now carries a native `title` (a blank face reads "Blank: rolls for 2 block." when it has no other hover text) — the existing custom `.hover-tip` overlay is untouched, this is additive.

## Stage 2.73 (BUILD 146)

Stage 2.73 (BUILD 146) — UI adjustments and art loading, five independent items, no number and no mechanic changed. (1) CLAUDE.md trim: CURRENT SUBSTAGE cut to one line per build (the previous build's own detail moved to HISTORY.md), CONFIRMED WORKING's oldest 140 build entries collapsed to eight summary lines — CLAUDE.md dropped from 79,404 to 66,655 bytes before this build's own edits. (2) Window scaling: applyScale() (js/rendering.js) sets document.documentElement.style.zoom to Math.min(2, Math.max(1, 1.1 * Math.min(window.innerWidth/1600, window.innerHeight/900))), called once from init() and again on every window resize — 1.1 at 1600x900, 2 (capped) at 3200x1800, 1 (floored) at 1200x675. html/body's height:100vh became height:100% (with a new html{height:100%} rule) so this chain shrinks the same way percentage-based widths already do — vh units ignore a zoomed ancestor and would otherwise overflow the viewport by the zoom factor. (3) End Turn/cards: #endTurnBtn is 128x44 (was 150x56), font-size 11px, align-self:center on the hand row; .hand-card-el is 144x216 (was 130x196), its art placeholder 100px tall, name 12px, effect text 20px; .band-c is 228px tall (was 208px); .side-col gap is 10px (was 3px). (4) Intent icon hover: renderIntentIcon() (js/rendering.js) now takes an optional sentence argument and every renderEnemyIntent() branch passes its own valueEl.title through to #enemyIntentIcon's title/aria-label and the inline SVG's own <title>, so hovering the icon reads the same sentence as the number. (5) Character art: #playerArtBox/#enemyArtBox each hold an img (#playerArtImg/#enemyArtImg) plus a text label (#playerArtLabel/#enemyArtLabel) — renderArtBoxes()'s new setArtImage() helper sets img.src to art/ordained.png or art/ + gameState.enemy.name.toLowerCase() + '.png', hides the label on the img's own load event and hides the img (showing the label) on its error event; no art files ship this build, so the label is what actually renders. art/ (with .gitkeep) and its own guardrail root-list entry are new; audio/ is unchanged.

Verification: facts 111/111, mods 40/40, build141 22/22, build142 22/22, guardrails 19/19, build144 13/13, build145 23/23 (two of its own assertions updated for zoom-related Chromium getComputedStyle quirks and the art box's new label child — see tests/build145.test.js), new tests/build146.test.js 11/11. tests/mods.test.js, tests/autoplay.js and tests/screenshots.js each gained the same one-line filter, ignoring the expected "Failed to load resource" console message the missing art files produce. Screenshots regenerated at 1600x900; diff vs the previous build's set is 11-14% per screen, expected from the 1.1x window-scaling zoom enlarging every element; 0 console errors, 0 page errors.

## Stage 2.74 (BUILD 147)

Stage 2.74 (BUILD 147) — four independent fixes from Fergus's first look at the previous build, no number and no mechanic changed. (1) Intent hover box: #enemyIntentIcon and #enemyIntentValue each carry position:relative and a .hover-tip child (index.html), shown on hover via the same box other die rows use (game-font, opens downward). renderIntentIcon() (js/rendering.js) builds the icon's own .hover-tip span inline with its SVG; renderEnemyIntent() routes every branch through a shared setValue() helper that writes the same sentence into both the icon's and the value's .hover-tip children, and calls renderIntentIcon() with that sentence — never the DOM title attribute. The native title attribute, and the SVG's own inner <title> element, are gone from both elements. (2) Console filter: tests/autoplay.js, tests/mods.test.js, tests/screenshots.js and tests/build146.test.js now drop a "Failed to load resource" console error only when the failing resource's own path (msg.location().url — msg.text() itself never carries the URL) is under art/; any other missing resource (a script, font, audio file) still fails the test. (3) Rolled face holds: js/rendering.js's renderDieList() gives a blank rolled face the same die-row-rolled sustained class a loaded face gets on every render after its own flash render (D-10) — previously a blank got only its flash class and nothing more. (3) The Font: an unresolved rollWithRelics() roll (D-21) — Nat 20 opens the Load panel + gold, a loaded face +1 weight, a blank grants gold, Nat 1 costs HP floored at 1.

## Stage 2.80 (BUILD 153) — former CURRENT SUBSTAGE

Stage 2.80 (BUILD 153) — four items, all kept.

(A) The rename. Every relic is an artifact: run.artifacts, config.artifacts, hasArtifact(), rollWithArtifacts(), ARTIFACT_MAX, the artifactReward* panel and its functions, #artifactRow/.artifact-slot/#artifactRewardPanel, the [ARTIFACT] log prefix and transcript line, hover text, test names and this file. The three existing artifacts keep their ids and effects; the row grew from five slots to eight, ARTIFACT_MAX 5 to 8. tests/build153.test.js fails if the old word reappears under js/, index.html or tests/.

(B) Ten artifacts, thirteen in all, defs in config.artifacts, amounts in GAME_CONFIG.ARTIFACTS: Tithe Box, Merchant's Seal, Leaden Face, Reliquary Chain, Plague Bell, Alms, Hourglass, Second Chance, Gilded Die, Bone Counter. Each with a hook of its own registers a permanent listener in init() and gates on hasArtifact(); the other four gate at the shop price or roll-path call site they act on. New: the FIGHT_START hook (beginFightFromSlot(), Plague Bell's), turn.boundTriggeredThisRound/enemyRoundSkippedThisTurn/gildedFace/secondChanceUsedThisFight, #secondChanceBtn and #gildedDieBtn on the roll strip, and rollDie()'s one read of turn.gildedFace.

(C) Artifacts in the shop: one slot at SHOP.ARTIFACT_PRICE (150), drawn from the artifacts not held, discounted by Merchant's Seal like every price but the removal. The reward panel already drew from the unheld; with thirteen it offers three until eleven are held.

(D) Seven cards, pool 41 to 48 (tiers 25/16/7): Venom, Ballast, Refrain, Second Sight, Cadence, Watchword, Blight Weight.

Stage 2.81 (BUILD 154) — three items, all kept.

(A) One reward panel shape (D-86) — renderOfferPanel() (rendering.js), written up in SCREEN LAYOUT / POST-FIGHT OVERLAY. #dieActionDieList, #eventDieList, .die-action-title and .die-action-empty keep their names, so every test that indexes them still does.

(B) Pop numbers (D-87, F46), written up in SCREEN LAYOUT / POP NUMBERS. The trap worth naming: applyScale() zooms <html>, so getBoundingClientRect() comes back already multiplied while style.left reads in the zoomed context's own pixels — spawnFxNumber() divides that back out, or a pop lands off its readout.

(C) #buildStamp reads DIE V1 — BUILD N from GAME_CONFIG.BUILD alone; the hand-typed, three-builds-stale stage number is gone.

# BUILD 155 — full write-up (moved from CLAUDE.md CURRENT SUBSTAGE by BUILD 156)

Stage 2.82 (BUILD 155) — four items, all kept.

(A) KI-31 — enterSlot() marks the slot entered in gameState.run.act before its handler runs and refuses (logged) a second call on an already-entered slot; an entered current node reads as completed (no click handler) instead of current; no map node, dev-jump included, accepts a click while the reward layer is open. Written up in MAP SCREEN.

(B) Roll odds — rollOdds() (pipeline.js) reads the exact bag rollDie() builds (Gilded Die's extra tickets included) and returns each face's tickets/total/floored percent. The face row's own caption (die-face-caption) shows that percent instead of the bare weight number; the weight itself lives only in the square's title now (faceTitleText() already printed it). GAME_CONFIG.ODDS_EMPHASIS (DROP_PX 6, COLOUR #e8e4d0) sets a weight-above-1 face's own emphasis. Written up in DIE COLUMN.

(C) The reward layer — die action/card/artifact/shop/The Font moved inside #fightScreen, right before band-d; #fightScreen.reward-layer-active hides band-b/band-c/roll-hero/die icons so the layer covers everything above the fight's own face row (#playerDieList), which a face-picking step now wires directly (currentPlayerDiePickConfig(), rendering.js) — no second die row anywhere (#dieActionDieList/#eventDieList are gone). #mapScreen hides the same way so the layer shows in place of the map too. Written up in THE REWARD LAYER (renamed from POST-FIGHT OVERLAY).

(D) CLAUDE.md headroom — the previous build's CURRENT SUBSTAGE write-up moved to HISTORY.md verbatim (the standing SIZE RULE mechanism); this file back under the 72,000-byte ceiling.
